- **ask creaite opens over the page (BL-173, sprint-polish-2; replaces BL-150's making room for the chat alone).** `#chat` is a layer over the page at every width, with its shadow; the page keeps its width and layout and nothing reflows when the chat opens, closes or is resized, and `wfFit()` no longer folds because of it. The item panel (`#panel`, reading and editing) is unchanged in every respect: while it is open with the chat at 1100px and wider, the rules below still apply (`body:has(#chat.on):has(.panel.on)`), so the panel and its Save sit beside the chat exactly as before; below 1100px the editing panel keeps BL-80's `margin-right` for the chat. Review's narrow-row wrap (BL-157) applies only in that case too.
- **The ask creaite panel makes room (BL-150; since BL-173 only while the item panel is open too).** At 1100px and wider, while `#chat` and `#panel` are open, `.shell` (rail, bar, page and side panel) takes `margin-right:var(--chatw)`, so the page sits beside the panel and follows its dragged width; the chat loses its drop shadow there. The side panel's wide (editing) width becomes half of what is left (`max(420px, (100vw - chatw - 72px) * .5)`) rather than half the window, and its old `margin-right` for the chat is dropped. Below 1100px the chat stays a layer, as before. Wide tables keep scrolling inside their frame (`.tbl`). While the side panel is open (or under 1100px), the Work filter bar does not wrap onto extra rows: `wfFit()` (on render and on a ResizeObserver of `#v-work`) folds the unused filter buttons from the end (priorities, projects, owners, due; Status and any filter that is set always stay) into **+ Filter**, whose picker lists every field; + Filter's tooltip says how many are there.

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **Today without a calendar is one line (BL-151).** When there is no calendar for today, `renderDay` hides the timeline (`#dayLine`) and `#dayMeets` holds one line, `.daynone`: "No calendar yet" (or "No calendar for today yet", with when it was last read, when an older calendar is saved) and **Refresh calendar** (`[data-refcal]`, Intake's `refreshCalendar`; disabled without the live server). When the calendar arrives the band opens as before; a calendar with no meetings today keeps its line and the headline says "No meetings today".

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **The tab title (BL-152).** `tabTitle()` sets `document.title` to `creaite · <where>`: the page's name (Today, Intake, Projects, Work, People, Review, Files, Activity, Settings, Help), a project's name on its page, a person's name on theirs; on Review it adds what waits there, `creaite · Review (4)` (not Intake's count). The finished-run mark (BL-139) and the VIP count (BL-99) still go first, and `DEV · ` in the development repository (`BASETITLE`). It runs after `go`, `renderBadge` (the count), `vipBadge`, `renderPerson` and `renderCockpit`.

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **Work says why it opened on a narrowed view (BL-153).** Work opens each tab on the view Settings › Work and views › Opening views names (shipped default: All open). When that is not the tab's first view, `#wapplied` starts with `.openedon`: "Opened on **Overdue**, as set in Settings · **Show all open**" (Settings opens that section; Show all open applies the first view). It is shown only for the view a tab opened on by itself, and goes as soon as the view changes; a view Alan picks is never explained.

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **No dead ends into Claude Code (BL-149).** The rule: if the dashboard can do it, it does; the Claude Code command is a footnote shown only when the live server is down. (1) A report reminder on Today has one primary button on its row, **Draft weekly report** or **Draft client report** (`remBtn`, `data-d="run"`: the same `runReminder` as the … menu's Run report now, which sends the command to ask creaite; disabled with "Needs the live server", "Running…" while it runs); its body line is empty while the server runs and says "It needs the live server; or say “…” in Claude Code" when it doesn't. (2) **Approve** on a draft in Review already goes through `review.py` (`reviewViaServer` → `/api/review`), one draft at a time; **Open draft** says "then press Approve here"; Approve all routine still never covers minutes. (3) A **conflict** row shows each field's current value beside the proposed one ("Now: … · This one: …") and **Keep current** / **Use this one** in place of Approve (`review.py keep` / `override` through `/api/review`, then the usual apply with Undo); pressing A on it points to the two buttons. (4) Other places: "Already applied" messages point to Activity's Undo when the server runs; People's "names still in settings.json" line offers **Move them into the register** (ask creaite, which asks first).

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **The edit panel shows the fields that matter (BL-154; for existing work items, Details in the peek since BL-176; new items keep More fields).** For actions, RAID items and decisions, `drawPanel` (wrapped) asks `fieldsFor` for the fields in two parts and moves the second into `<details class="morefields">` "More fields (n)" at the end of the form, before the Record section. Actions: Action, Project, Owner, Priority, Due date, Status, Latest update | Workstream, Raised, Closed, Milestone, Client messaging. RAID: Description, Type, Project, Owner, the response, Impact, Likelihood, Priority, Due date, Status | Workstream, Raised, Date closed, Milestone, Client messaging. Decisions: Decision, Project, Status, Decision maker, Needed by, Date made, (Source), Reasoning | Options considered, Revisit, Replaces, Client messaging. More fields opens by itself when one of its fields has a value beyond the defaults (Raised and an unticked client box don't count), when a validation message is on one of them, or when that field is the one asked for (`drawPanel(focusField)`); a section Alan opened stays open while that record is redrawn. Each part keeps the original order, so Tab follows the screen; `readForm`, validation, ⌘↵ and Esc read every field wherever it sits; inline cell edits don't use the panel and are unchanged. Milestones, health and proposals' update cards are unchanged.

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **The Keys chip (BL-155; removed from Work and Review by BL-175, sprint-peek: the keys are in Help's table).** Work and Review each have a **Keys** chip (`#wKeys`, `#rvKeys`, `.keyschip`) at the end of their toolbar, after Display. It opens a popover (`.keysmenu`, the `.ddmenu` motion) listing the hints of the list in use, one per line (the List's or the Board's, All items' read-only line, Review's), with "Press ? for every shortcut"; Esc or a click elsewhere closes it. Help's keyboard shortcuts (the `?` key) list the same keys. The permanent hint lines (`#whintList`, `#whintBoard`, `#rvHint`) stay until the chip has been opened once in this browser (`localStorage` `creaite-keys-seen`, then `body.keys-seen` hides them).

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **First run: a checklist on Today (BL-156).** On a vault that hasn't finished set-up (no project, nobody in the people register, or no sweep yet: `D.last_sweep`), `#tBrief` starts with `#setupList` ("Getting started n of 5"), before Needs attention. Five steps, each ticked as it is done and, until then, with one button (the first undone one primary): **Connect Microsoft 365** (ticked by the first sweep, which needs it; "Show me how" opens Help's Start here, since it is done in Claude Code), **Add a project** (Settings › Projects and register), **Add people** (Settings › People), **Run the first sweep** (sends "sweep" to ask creaite; needs the live server), **Open Review** (ticked once Review has been opened in this browser). It goes when all five are ticked; **Dismiss** hides it for good by adding `setup:checklist` to `reminders.dismissed_forever`, so Settings › Reminders › **Restore** brings it back. Nothing it does is new. Help's first hour, Start the day and Opening the dashboard (and so the first-day guide) offer the **creaite** launcher first, with Terminal as the fallback.

## The UX baseline (BL-146 to BL-157; sprint-ux)
- **UX sweep fixes (BL-157).** An empty Activity says "Nothing has changed yet…" with Open Review; an empty Projects page offers **Add a project** (the checklist's action); Quick capture's unsent note is kept in `localStorage` (`creaite-qc-draft`) and restored on load (the form opens), cleared by Clear and Process (`qcClear`); Activity's older applies read "Applied" (title: only the latest apply can be undone) instead of "Use “undo last apply”"; Restore dismissed reminders also clears the page's copy, so the set-up checklist comes back at once. While ask creaite is open (up to 1500px), Review's action cells may wrap so their buttons stay in sight.

## Icons in place of words (BL-158 to BL-161; sprint-icons)
- **The rule.** Icon only, with a tooltip and an `aria-label` (the same words): familiar utility or navigation actions in a toolbar or a row (layout switch, Display, filter, copy, edit, rename, remove, undo, restore, refresh, preview, open folder, open, close, clear, collapse, stop, send, date picker, theme). Icon and a short word: actions that start work or that Alan reads as a decision (Approve, Approve routine, Weekly report, Client report, Draft email, Hand over, Prep page, One-pager). Words only: Cancel, Save, Create, Discard, Install, Undo update, the conflict choices, Dismiss, the snackbar's Undo, and every button not in the legend (Keys, Refresh on Files, Select new, Process selected, Copy diagnostics…). A field-name filter button (Project, Owner, Status) keeps its words; the left rail is unchanged.
- **One set (BL-158).** `ICONS` (path strings on a 24px grid) and `ic(name, o)` (1.8 stroke, round caps, `currentColor`, `aria-hidden`; `o` is a class or `{cls, w, size, bare}`). Static markup writes `<svg data-ic="name" …>` and `paintIcons()` fills it, so the rail, header and section icons keep their own attributes and look. `ibtn(icon, label, attrs, cls, key)` draws an icon-only button (`.ib`, 32px; `.sm` 28px; `.bd` bordered). The typed glyphs are gone from controls: the date button is the calendar icon (`CALI = ic("calendar")`), close and clear are the x, collapse is one chevron that turns (`svg.chev`, rotated −90° when `aria-expanded="false"`), the theme button shows the sun in the dark theme and the moon in the light (`themeIcon`, after `applyTheme` and the system's change), with "Switch to light/dark theme". Not changed: the breadcrumb ›, "see all ›" links, the calendar's and the project switcher's ‹ ›, the Current state fold, the Board's ⋯, the cell-edit ▾, a saved view's ▾ and More views ▾, Settings › Advanced's › (already turning), the health and item-type icon maps (`HI`, `KI`) and ask creaite's own spark.
- **One tooltip (BL-158).** `#tip` (`role="tooltip"`, inverse colours, the theme's tokens) for any element with `data-tip`; `data-key` adds the key (only one the page handles: D, F, E, A, R, X, Shift+R, J, K, Esc, Enter). Hover shows it after 250ms (at once when moving from one icon to the next), keyboard focus (`:focus-visible`) at once; Esc, scrolling, a press, leaving or the control going hides it; the pointer can move onto it (WCAG 1.4.13). Below the control, or above it near the bottom of the window. When the tip's words differ from the name it is linked with `aria-describedby`. `tipUp` gives every icon-only button drawn anywhere (an icon, no words, an `aria-label`) the same tooltip from its label, takes away its `title` and keeps a key written as "(Esc)".
- **Work toolbar (BL-159).** Search, the field buttons (words), the funnel `#moreBtn` (tooltip "Add a filter", F; a count badge `.dot` when filters are on, "Add a filter: 2 on"; when filters fold into it, "… 3 more filters are here"), the List/Board switch `#wlay` (two icons, `aria-pressed`, filled when on; the copy in the Display menu is gone), Display `#dispBtn` (sliders, "Display: grouping, order and properties", D), Keys (words), and at the right end the **New tile** `#newBtn` (style A: a 32px accent-soft tile with an accent plus; on hover, focus or while its menu is open it turns solid accent and grows to show its label, `.w`: New action, New RAID item, New decision; the label is its `aria-label` and tooltip; hidden on All items). On Decisions the tile opens `#newMenu` (`role="menu"`, ↓ ↑ Home End, Esc returns focus, Tab or a click away closes): **Record a decision** (`openNew("decisions")`: Made, today) and **Ask for a decision** (`openNew("decisions","needed")`: Pending). Since BL-179 `openNew` opens the empty peek, not the Create form. `#newDecNeeded` is gone.
- **Review (BL-160).** Funnel `#rvMore` (count of Review's own filters; no key: F is Work's), Display `#rvDispBtn` (sliders), **Approve routine** (double tick and the word; its tooltip says what stays for one-by-one approval; the preview, BL-133, unchanged). The bulk bar keeps **Approve (n)** and **Reject (n)** with their icons; Reject with a reason is a chevron (Shift+R), clear an x (Esc). Rows: **Approve** keeps its word with a tick; Reject is an x (R, the `--bad` hover); Edit or Open draft (pencil, E) and Refresh items (circular arrow) sit in `.rowact`, shown on hover, on focus within the row and on the cursor row.
- **The rest (BL-161).** Project page foot: **Weekly report** and **Client report** (page icon and the word; named "Draft weekly report", "Draft client report") and Browse files (folder). Person page: Edit details (pencil), **Draft email** (envelope), **Hand over…** (bent arrow). Prep: **Prep page** (the spark), Open last prep page (arrow out of a box), **One-pager** and **Client-safe one-pager** (page icon). Rows: Files' Open folder, Intake's Preview (eye) and Remove from Intake (bin), Settings › People and the register lists' Edit details, Rename (T), Restore (curved arrow), Remove from list or Retire (bin), Activity's Undo this change and Undo this apply, Settings' Copy the command (ticks for a second; `copied()` calls `icTick` for an icon button), Check now (circular arrow) and Reset column widths and order (curved arrow). ask creaite: Send is a round accent button with an up arrow (Enter) that becomes a stop square (Esc) while a run is going (`setRunning`); close is the x.

## Side panel: an unsaved edit survives a reload (BL-167, sprint-polish-2)

**(For actions, RAID items and decisions, replaced by the work item peek: BL-176, sprint-peek, below.)** New items, milestones, rebaselining and project health keep it.

- While an action, RAID item or decision (existing or new) is being edited, the fields changed from what the form opened with are kept in `localStorage` `creaite-panel-drafts`, keyed `<kind>:<ID>` or `new:<kind>`, with the time and the item's `updated_at` (the 30 most recent). Nothing is staged until Save.
- Opening that item (or New of that kind) shows a bar at the top of the panel (`#pDraft`): "Unsaved changes to <ID> from DD-MM-YYYY HH:MM (n fields), kept in this browser." with **Restore unsaved changes** (into edit mode, fields filled, More fields opened when one is there) and **Discard**. Typing afresh without choosing replaces the old draft.
- A draft over 7 days old, or whose `updated_at` no longer matches the item's, is dropped when the item is opened, with "… were dropped: <why>." and **OK**.
- Save, Cancel, Esc and Close end the edit and drop the draft; only a reload or a closed tab keeps it. Milestones, project health and proposals in Review are not covered.

## Wide tables show that they scroll (BL-165, sprint-polish-2)

- Any table frame (`.tbl`) in the main area whose table is wider than the frame (and which scrolls: `overflow-x` not `visible`) gets `.xscroll` and a zero-height `.xcue` block as its first child, sticky at the frame's top left, holding a 40px fade at the right edge, a 16px soft edge beside the first column once scrolled from the start, and a **more columns →** button (top right, over the header row) that scrolls right by 80% of the frame (smoothly unless motion is reduced). The fade and the button go when the frame is scrolled to its last column. A table that fits gets nothing.
- The first column is sticky (`.xstick`, `left:0`, the surface behind it; row highlight, selection and hover still colour it) when it is at most half the frame's width; a wider first column (Work's title column on a narrow frame) scrolls with the rest.
- Kept current by a `MutationObserver` on `.content`, a `ResizeObserver`, the frame's own scroll, and Work's `sizeList`, which now also runs when `#workWrap` itself is resized (the item panel opening narrows it without a window resize), so the list scrolls inside its frame instead of running under the panel. Help's tables are left alone.

## Help's loop diagram reflows by its own width (BL-168, sprint-polish-2)

- `.hp-loop` is a size container; below 900px of its own width (`@container (max-width:900px)`) the five steps stack in one column with the arrows turned down, as the old 860px window rule did. The step numbers and the gate label are at least 12px (`max(12px,.72rem)`). The rule is inside the Help CSS section, so the generated guides carry it. Since BL-173 the chat doesn't narrow Help, so the diagram doesn't reflow when it opens.

## Set-up in the dashboard (BL-166, sprint-polish-2)

- **Settings › System › Set-up** (`#suCard`, section id `setup`, after Live server and backups): **Run the health check** (`/api/setup/run {step:"health"}`: doctor.py; the summary line, then WARN and FAIL lines, the passes folded in a `<details>`); **Rebuild…** (shows what it rewrites, `#suConfirm`, then **Rebuild now**: `{step:"rebuild"}`: build_dashboard.py, then excel_sync.py export only when `changed` finds no unimported edits); **Microsoft 365** (Connected with the last sweep's date, or one line, the text to say in Claude Code, `setup: check Microsoft 365`, with Copy and Check again); **Launcher** (Made with its place, or one line and the Terminal command `cd <vault> && python3 system/scripts/launch.py make-launcher`, with Copy and Check again); **The rest of set-up** (`setup`, with Copy).
- **The checklist (BL-156):** Connect Microsoft 365's row says in one line why the page can't, with Copy, Check again and How (Help); a line under the list links to Set-up. Check again calls `/api/setup/status`: a recorded sweep counts as connected (the page never connects to Microsoft 365), and the row ticks.
- **The server (live_server.py, `===== BL-166 =====`):** `/api/setup/status` (read only: the last sweep, a launcher in `~/Applications/creaite.app` or `creaite.command` in the vault, which steps may run) and `/api/setup/run {step}` (only `health` or `rebuild`; each command must already match ask creaite's allow list, READ_TOOLS for a read and READ_TOOLS + WRITE_TOOLS for a change, and no DENY_TOOLS rule; run by the server's own `run()` under `LOCK`, as Apply is). `launch.py make-launcher` matches no rule, so it is never run by the page.
## Sprint rail (BL-162 to BL-164; sprint-rail)

- **The Current state card's title (BL-162).** The card's `<h2>` (`#csHead`, the card's `aria-labelledby`) holds a button `.cstbtn` (`data-cst=<CODE>`) with the title and a pencil that shows on hover and focus; tooltip "Rename this title" (BL-158's `data-tip`). Click or Enter turns it into `input.cstin` (one line, `maxlength` 40, label "Title of this card", the words selected): Enter or blur saves, Esc cancels and puts focus back on the button. Empty, or the default words in any case, mean "Current state". Kept per project as `- **Current state title:** <words>` in the project's `CLAUDE.md`, under the client-report rhythm line (added there, or directly above `## Current state` when there is no rhythm line, and removed for the default); `build_dashboard.memory()` reads it as `cstitle` (`cs_title`: trimmed, Markdown and line breaks out, 40 characters, default "Current state"). Saved like `setRhythm`: `needVault`, a copy at `system/backups/files/<CODE>-CLAUDE-<stamp>.md`, the line, and `settings-log.jsonl` key `current_state_title.<CODE>`; the snack's Undo writes the copy back (or, if the file changed since, only the line). Not editable without the live server or while the Current state editor is open. The title also names the Generate dialog (`Generate: <title>`) and the editor's panes (`<title>, left pane`). The `## Current state` section, the reports, Generate, search and Activity are unchanged.
- **The left rail, customised (BL-163; the sidebar since BL-319 part 6, below).** Built from `RAIL_ITEMS` and `dashboard.rail` in `settings.json`. Superseded in detail by "The sidebar (BL-319 part 6)": the hide switches, the "…" button for hidden pages and the `#railPop` popover are gone; a page that was hidden now sits in More.
- **The sidebar (BL-319 part 6, v1.9.2).** Preact components (`dev/COMPONENTS.md`) in `src/components/sidebar/` (`RailToggle`, `RailNav`, `RailButton`, `RailMore`, `CustomiseSidebar`, `RailIcon` with the icons as element data), reading `src/stores/rail.js` (signals: `railCfg`, `railView`, `railCounts`, `railMoreOpen`, `railCustOpen`, `railNarrow`, `railCollapsed`, `railMoreWaiting`). `rail.js` mounts them into the shell's `#railTogBox` (in `.rhead`, before the wordmark), `#railMain`, `#railFoot` and `#railCustBox`; old code reaches the sidebar only through `setRailView(key)` (`go`, the milestones and meeting pages) and `setRailCount(id, n, label)` (`renderBadge`, `inboxBadge`, `vipBadge`), and the current page, the counts and the names are bound to the signals, so they change at once. `shared.RAILCFG` is an accessor onto `railCfg`.
  - **Saved:** `dashboard.rail = {order, more, hidden, display}`: `order` the pages at the top (Today always first), `more` the pages in More, `hidden` kept for the shape (always `[]`; a page an older version hid goes into More), `display` `titles` or `icons` (collapsed). Defaults in `settings-defaults.json`: top Today, Work, Review, Projects, People; More Intake, Files, Activity, Settings. `railNorm` keeps unknown ids out, puts each page in one list once, keeps Today first and out of More, appends a page in neither list to its default group, and gives a choice saved before More existed the default More plus the pages it hid. `railSave` draws at once, writes with `writeSettings` (a backup and a log line) and puts it back if the write fails.
  - **Expanded:** 212px (`--rail-w`), each page an icon and its name in a 36px row, the count as a badge at the row's end, the current page with `aria-current="page"`, a tinted row and the 3px accent bar. **Collapsed:** `:root.railicons`, `.rail.collapsed`, 56px; names hidden, each button's `aria-label` is its name with its count ("Review, 4 waiting") and `data-tip` its name (the one tooltip, placed beside the sidebar); the badge sits on the icon. Under 1200px the sidebar is always collapsed and the toggle hidden (`railNarrow`, nothing saved), so the 212px sidebar never squeezes the page, the side peek or the top bar on a smaller screen (BL-80, BL-81, BL-176 keep their widths).
  - **The toggle** (`#railTog`): the panel icon at the top-left, before the wordmark, at the same place either way; `aria-label` and tooltip say what it will do ("Collapse the sidebar" or "Expand the sidebar"), with the key (⌘\ on a Mac, Ctrl+\ elsewhere); `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls="rail"`. ⌘\ (Ctrl+\) toggles anywhere. Saved as `display`.
  - **More** (`#railMore`, hidden when More is empty): at the bottom; `aria-haspopup="menu"`, `aria-expanded`; its badge (`#railMoreBadge`) adds up the counts of the pages inside and its name says which ("More: Intake has 2 waiting"). `#railMoreMenu` (`role=menu`, `position:fixed`) opens upwards, or beside the rail when collapsed, focus on its first item; ↑ ↓ (wrapping), Home, End; Esc closes and focus returns to More; Tab or a click away closes; choosing a page opens it. Its items are the pages (`role=menuitem`, with their badges, which stay in the page while it is closed), a separator and **Customise sidebar…** (`#railCustBtn`).
  - **Customise sidebar** (`#railCust`, a modal `<dialog>` named by its heading): two lists, "In the sidebar" and "In More" (`ul[data-zone]`); each page a row (`li[data-rid]`) with a drag handle (pointer events, within a list or across; never above Today), Move up, Move down and Move to More or Move to the sidebar (`[data-mv]`, disabled at the ends), and Alt with ↑ ↓ (within) or ← → (across) on a row; focus stays on the control used, each move is said in a polite live region. Today has no controls ("Always first"); Settings can live in either. **Icons only** (`role=switch`), **Reset to default**, **Cancel**, **Done** (saves). Esc, Cancel, or a click on the backdrop with nothing changed close it without saving; focus returns to where it was opened from. Opened from More, a right-click on the sidebar (Shift+F10 on a page in it), Settings › General › Appearance › Sidebar: Customise… (`#railCustSet`, `[data-railcust]`) and ⌘K "Customise the rail" (`railPopOpen`, an alias of `railCustomise`).
- **Activity: type icons, search, undo several (BL-164).** `build_dashboard.activity()` gives each line `kind` (action; Risk, Issue, Assumption or Dependency by the RAID type; decision, milestone, project, update, workstream, promise, label; file for Current state edits, rejected for reasons) and, when `tracker.py undo-changes` could put it back, the change log's `rowid`; a line put back by a revert still in force is `reverted` (shown struck through, "undone" in place of Undo); a revert apply (`via` "revert:<rowids>") is one line "Undid n changes" (`kind` revert) with the apply's own Undo when it is the latest. `activity_capped` says the list stops short (300 lines or the newest 2000 log rows; the footer `#actCap` says so). Page: `ACTKINDS` (kind, name, icon from `ic()`; the item-type shapes `KI` joined the set as action, risk, issue, assumption, dependency, decision, milestone, plus label), `.acticon` before the ID (`role=img`, `aria-label` and `data-tip` the type's name). Toolbar `#actTools`: `#actQ` (the `/` key focuses it while Activity is open; elsewhere `/` is still ask creaite), `#actCount` "n of N", `#actClear`, `#actFrom`/`#actTo` (BL-147 dates), `#actTypes` chips (kinds present, `aria-pressed`). Filters `ACTF` in the address: `#activity&q=…&type=Risk,action&from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=…` (a `?` after activity is read too). Empty: "Nothing matches “…” in the changes shown here" with Clear. Selection: BL-122's `HL` with k "act" (`hlOrder` knows `#actTbl tr[data-row]`; `hlPick`, `hlToggle`, `hlClear`), a tick box per choosable line, Esc clears first; `#actBar` "n selected: Undo n" and clear. Undo: a selection that is exactly the latest apply's lines calls `undoApply`; otherwise `/api/undo-changes {rowids, dry_run:true}` builds `#actDlg` (each line "ID · field from → to", "removed", "put back" or "skipped: why"; Undo n / Cancel; over 25 asks again), then `/api/undo-changes {rowids}`, `reloadData`, and the snack "Undid 3 changes, skipped 1" with Undo (the new apply).

## The work item peek (BL-176, BL-177; sprint-peek)

The design is `dev/mockups/work-item-peek-mockup.html`. Code: `system/templates/dashboard.html`, blocks marked `===== BL-176 (sprint-peek) =====` and `===== BL-177 (sprint-peek) =====` (CSS and JS, at the end of each).

- **Where it lives:** the same `#panel`, with the class `peek` whenever `pkIs()` (an action, RAID item or decision, `CUR.mode` "record", "proposal" for BL-177, or "new" for BL-179). The outermost `drawPanel` wrapper draws the peek (`pkDraw`) and leaves every other panel (milestones, rebaseline, project health, update proposals) to the old code. The old `.ph`, `.pb`, `.pf` are hidden in peek mode.
- **Layout:** `position:fixed`, right edge of the window, `width:max(520px,50vw)` (never wider than the window less the rail), z-index 40, over the page: the page never narrows or moves (BL-173's "make room" rules and `wfFit` exclude `.peek`). With ask creaite open: `right` is the chat's width and the width is `max(520px,(100vw - chatw)/2)`; where that would run under the rail, the peek keeps its 520px and the chat sits over its right part, and `--pkover` pads the peek's toolbar, content and footer by the overlap so nothing is out of reach. `.pkwide` (the expand icon) takes the full width left of the chat. Slides in over 160ms (`translateX(28px)`, opacity), none under reduced motion.
- **Keys:** Esc closes a picker, then puts back a text field's text, then (after BL-122's Esc that clears a highlighted row) closes the peek; from inside ask creaite it closes the chat and leaves the peek. Closing the peek blurs whatever has focus in it, so a text field commits. ⌘↵ works in the peek, its pickers, the pill and the list behind it (not in another text box or the chat).
- **Toolbar (`.pk-bar`):** Close (arrow, Esc), Expand, Previous (K) and Next (J) through `hlOrder()` (Work, Mine, a person's page; disabled elsewhere), the save state (`#pkSave`: Saving…, Saved to staging, Applied, Not saved), Sneak peek (`#pkSneakBtn`, //; BL-322, below), Draft chase (`#pkChaseBtn`, C; BL-298, below), Copy link (`actHref`), More (Mark done or Mark as made, ask creaite about this, Copy ID).
- **Body (`#pkScroll`):** the ID and project link (and RAID's Type chip), early warnings, the title (the one editor in its plain form, one line; `role="textbox"`, named by the field: Action, Description or Decision), the key row (`.pk-props`, chips `[data-pkp]` with `aria-label` "Field: value"), Impact and Likelihood (RAID), a note line (`#pkNote`, a rule's message), the free text (Latest update, RAID's response by type, Reasoning), the "Last edited" line, **Details** (`#pkDetT`, `#pkDet`) and the feed (`#pkTabs`, `#pkFeed`).
- **Text:** a tint on hover and focus, never a box; commits on leaving the field (`focusout`), Enter ends a one-line field (title, Revisit), Esc puts the text back. A redraw never replaces the text under the cursor, and keeps the focus on the same chip.
- **Pickers (`.pkpop`):** fixed layers at z-index 60 (over ask creaite), placed under the chip and kept inside the window (`pkPlace`). Lists (`pkList`): arrows, Home, End, Enter, Esc (back to the chip), Tab closes; the owner, workstream and Replaces lists search (`pkList`'s `filter` gives a list its own matching). The owner list matches through `ownerMatches`, the one matcher the old combo used (BL-187): a name, an alias or an email finds the person, a likely match is offered as "Is “Sara” Sara Khan?" (picking it remembers the alias, `regAddAlias`), and a stranger is "New person “…”…" (`ownerFinal`, the details dialog). The owner list ends with "Open <name>'s page" (My work for Alan), so the name still leads to the person (BL-56). Dates (`pkDate`): a Monday-first month (arrows move by day and week), Today, Tomorrow, Next Monday, In a week, Clear, and a box that takes DD-MM-YYYY or words (`parseWhen`), "Use DD-MM-YYYY, …" when it can't read it. A click away, a scroll of the peek or a window resize closes a picker.
- **Staging (`pkWrite`):** each commit writes one proposal (`proposed-<date>-<time>-<ms><n>-dashboard.json`, the staging format, `source_run: "dashboard"`, approved) through `/api/fs`, and is never applied on the spot, whatever `dashboard.auto_apply` says. Only an item's first staged change carries `based_on`. `PKS` (kept in `localStorage` `creaite-peek-staged`) lists them; after any reload of the data (`reloadData`, wrapped) each is checked against staging: gone means applied (marked in the feed), still there means still staged (shown again on the fresh data). **Every window (BL-188):** each proposal also carries a `peek` note (the item, the change in the page's fields, its words, `ms`); `build_dashboard.peek_staged` lists the unapplied ones in the page's data (`D.peek_staged`, through `/api/data` on each refresh), and `pkFromVault` adds those this window doesn't know to `PKS` on load and before each check, so the pill, the feed's "staged", the dots and the staged values show in any window, and Apply or Undo last works there. Only staged changes are in the files: text being typed elsewhere never shows. The pill (`#pkStage`, bottom centre of the page or of what the chat leaves): "N changes staged on M items", "Nothing reaches the tracker until you apply", **Undo last** (removes the newest of them only if its file is still in staging with no item applied; `/api/fs` remove allows only `-dashboard` proposals) and **Apply** (`/api/apply`, the existing apply; ⌘↵ shown on it). A dot (`.pkdot`) marks a staged row in the lists and on board cards.
- **Rules:** as BL-176 lists them (closed date set and cleared, made decisions, Date made, client-visible waits for complete messaging and stages both together, messaging of a client-visible item can't be cleared, empty title, Priority recalculated). ⌘↵ (Ctrl+↵) anywhere in the peek or its pickers: commit the field, close a picker, apply everything staged, close; an item waiting for messaging stays open with the reason; nothing staged closes with "Nothing to apply".
- **Details:** Project (a list; a milestone or workstream that doesn't belong to the new project is cleared, and said), Workstream (the project's own; **Add workstream…**, or "Add “typed” as a workstream…", opens a small form with BL-58's details suggested by `wsSuggest`, and **Add and use it** stages the new workstream ahead of the item's change in the same proposal through `wsNewOf`, the old panel's route: Undo last removes both; for a new item it rides in its one proposal while its workstream is that one; a proposal in Review keeps its row's own Add workstream; BL-187), Milestone, Options considered, Revisit, Replaces and Replaced by (decisions), Raised and Closed (read only), Client-visible (`role="switch"`) and Client messaging (the three headings for actions and RAID). Opens by itself for a client-visible item (or one waiting for messaging); otherwise `PK.det[id]` keeps Alan's choice for the session.
- **Feed:** All, Activity, Updates (changes to the free text), Source (the item's source and its creation). Lines are `ACT` (the change log as `build_dashboard.activity()` gives it, the Activity page's own data) for the item, plus its staged changes first ("staged"); a line from an apply made since the page loaded that took a staged change is marked "applied", and one undone since "undone". No Comments tab.
- **Review (BL-177):** `openProposal` for an action, RAID item or decision opens the same layout on `CUR.draft` (`pkX`, `pkCid` "rv<n>"). Edits stay in the draft; the footer (`#pkFoot2`) has Reject (`decide(n,"rejected")`), Cancel, Save and Save and approve, which run `savePanel` with the draft in place of the form (`readForm` is swapped for that call), so its validation, owner check, `norm` and `editProposal` are the same. ⌘↵ is Save. The "Editing a proposal" line, "Choose a project before saving.", the duplicate note and the Learn tick (`#f_learn`) are kept. `EDITING` stays on, as before (a click away asks to finish or cancel). Keys pressed in it stay in it (Review's A, R, X, E, J, K act on the list's cursor row otherwise). Copy link, More and the arrows are hidden for a proposal.
- **Retired for work items:** the read view, Edit and E, Save and Cancel, the Editing banner, More fields, the "⌘↵ saves and closes" line (BL-169), the unsaved draft (BL-167), Record the decision and Withdraw (Status does both; on the Board, Made opens the peek with Made staged), BL-80's half width and BL-136's width snap. Milestones, rebaseline and project health keep the form, BL-80 and BL-169; since BL-179 new work items are the peek too, and BL-167's draft store serves only an unstaged new item.

## Draft chase in the peek (BL-298; v19-draft-chase)

The design is `dev/mockups/draft-chase-mockup.html` (accepted by Alan, 13-10-2026: built as drawn). Code: `system/templates/dashboard/src/components/peek/ChasePane.jsx` (the pane, a Preact component, dev/COMPONENTS.md) and the block `===== BL-298 (v19-draft-chase) =====` at the end of `src/panel.js` (the icon, key C, mounting); CSS block `===== BL-298 (v19-draft-chase) =====` in `style.css`. The server side is `dev/archive/sprints/BL-298-server.md` (`POST /api/chase/pane`, `POST /api/chases`, `drafts.py chase_pane`).

- **The icon:** `#pkChaseBtn` (an envelope with an arrow; the icon is element data, `CHASE_ICON`, also put in `ICONS.chase` for `ibtn`), immediately left of Copy link; tooltip "Draft chase" with C. Shown only for an open action, risk or issue (Open or Delayed) and a Pending decision in `CUR.mode` "record" (`chaseCan`); hidden for closed, cancelled, made or withdrawn items, a proposal and a new item. **C** opens it while the peek is open, not while typing, in a menu, a picker, a dialog or ask creaite. `aria-pressed` while the pane is open; a second press goes back.
- **Inside the peek:** `#pkChase` (`.pk-scroll.pk-chase`) takes `#pkScroll`'s place; the toolbar stays. **Back** (focus starts on it) or Esc returns to the item and focus to the icon (Esc goes back before it closes the peek). Another item (J, K, the arrows, a row), a closed peek or an item no longer open puts the item back (`chaseDraw`, a `PKDRAW` step).
- **The pane, top to bottom:** Back and "Draft chase · <ID>"; the mode line (`mode_tag` as a tag, Yours in the ok colours; `mode_line` with its first words bold); **To** (a chip with the name, the email beside it; for Alan's own item the suggestions under it, each with its why, `aria-pressed` on the chosen one, and **+ Anyone**, a search over the people register less Alan and hidden people, eight at a time); for Alan's own item **To get … done** and **What I need from you**; **Reply by** (a date) beside **Tone** (First, Second, Firmer, `aria-pressed`); **Include the latest update**; the email with its To and Subject lines (a small **Polished** tag after the subject while a polished opening is in use); the Polish row (Alan, 13-10-2026, option A): one line saying what it does in the pane's Tone and **Polish the wording** (`#chPolish`, **Polish again** once one is in use; AI drafts off: `aria-disabled` with the reason under it, `#chPolWhy`), then while it runs "Polishing the wording…" with Stop, then the card: Yours beside Polished for the **Opening** and, for Alan's own item, Yours beside Suggested for **To get … done** and **What I need from you**, **Keep mine** or **Use this** (nothing changes until Use this). Tone maps First → warm, Second → neutral, Firmer → firm (`POLISH_TONES`); kind `polish` (opening only) or `polish-help` (Alan's own item, the two lines too), both through `polishAsk` and counted under `email`. Changing the tone or those two lines drops the polished wording. Last, the result line on the left, **Copy as text**, **I sent it** and **Copy for Outlook** (primary). No "VIP" anywhere (BL-288).
- **Drafting:** the pane asks `/api/chase/pane` with `{id, hour}` and Alan's choices (`to`, `tone`, `reply_by`, `include_update`, `outcome`, `need`, `cover`), 150 ms after the last change (a later reply wins); the controls start from the reply's `defaults` and `draft`. No model call.
- **The email:** `ChaseEmail` draws the reply's parts in the Draft email's fixed palette (`DE_COLOURS`, Calibri, `DE_FONT`) whatever the theme, every element styled inline (no classes or variables): the greeting, the opening, for help the highlighted **What I need from you:** line (`DE_COLOURS.askBg`, `askRule`), the one-row table (ID; the item with "Decision needed:" or "Risk:", the owner line for help, a RAID item's response and the latest update in grey; Due or Needed by with its words, red and bold when overdue, amber when due soon; priority, Urgent bold), the ask, the sign-off and Alan's first name. The page's own table look (`th` capitals, sticky headers) is reset inside `#chMail`.
- **Copy for Outlook:** the email's DOM serialised (`XMLSerializer`, so the page's HTML-sink count is unchanged) as `text/html` with the reply's plain text as `text/plain`, through `ClipboardItem`; **Copy as text**: the subject, a blank line and the plain text. A refused clipboard selects the email and says so. Copying records nothing; nothing is sent and no Outlook draft is made.
- **Polish:** `draftAsk({kind:"polish", text: draft.opening})`, behind Settings › AI drafts (`draftWhyNot("polish")` says why not); the text comes back as `cover`, so only the opening changes. **Use mine** clears it.
- **I sent it:** `/api/chases` `{ids:[id], mode}` (chase, help or decision); `SA().chases` takes the reply's counts; the result line "Recorded: chased <first> on <DD-MM-YYYY>. The next one will be a second (or firmer) reminder."; the tone goes back to the preset, which has now stepped up.
- **Wording (Alan, 13-10-2026):** chase mode is DE_CHASE's subjects with its texts in the singular for one item (`drafts.CHASE_WORDING["chase_one"]`); the greeting is the Draft email's "Good morning, <first>,"; help and decision modes sign off "Thank you," and Alan.

## Sneak peek (BL-322; v193-sneak-peek-ui)

The design is `dev/mockups/sneak-peek-mockup.html` (scope accepted by Alan, 14-10-2026: built as drawn). The server contract is `dev/sprints/BL-322-contract.md` (`POST /api/sneak/read`, plain code, read only). Code: `src/components/peek/SneakPeek.jsx` (the box, a Preact component, dev/COMPONENTS.md) and `src/sneak.js` (opening it, the keys, the hosts, the save path); CSS block `===== BL-322 (v193-sneak-peek-ui) =====` in `style.css`; one line in `milestones.js` (`mvEdDraw` calls `sneakMvAttach`), `panel.js` (Esc in `.sp-box` is the box's; `chaseOpen(start)`) and `ChasePane.jsx` (`start`).

- **Where:** the work-item peek (`#panel.peek`, an action, RAID item or decision in `CUR.mode` "record"; never a proposal or a new item) and the milestone editor (`#mvPeek`, not a new milestone). `sneakWhere()` says which. Not while Draft chase is open.
- **Opening:** `//` outside a field (`typing()` false, no dialog, not in ask creaite, a menu or a picker): a window-capture keydown takes the first `/` and waits 330 ms (`SLASH_WAIT`); a second `/` opens the box, otherwise the first is replayed on `document` so the page's own `/` (ask creaite; Activity's and Settings' search) works as before. The sparkle (`#pkSneakBtn`, `ICONS.sneak` from `SNEAK_ICON`, tooltip with `//`, `aria-pressed`) sits immediately left of Draft chase; in the milestone editor `#mvSneakBtn` sits before Close. The foot line (`#pkSneakHint`, `#mvSneakHint`: "// Change this item in words") sits under the scroll area, hidden while the box is open or Draft chase is. A second press of the sparkle closes it.
- **The box (`#spBox`, `role="dialog"`):** floats over the bottom of the peek (`.sp-host`, absolute, 12 px in); the scroll area's bottom padding follows the box's height (`--sp-h`, a ResizeObserver), so everything can be scrolled above it. Top to bottom: the output (`#spOut`, `aria-live="polite"`), then the input row (the sparkle, `#spIn` with a hidden label "Change <ID> in words", "Esc close"). Esc (handled by the box; the peek's and the milestone editor's Esc leave it alone) closes it and puts focus back where it was; ↑ in the empty box brings back the last instruction (`SNEAK_HIST`, kept across items). The milestone editor redraws itself whole, so `sneakMvAttach` re-appends the host after each draw and the box (and its focus) survives.
- **Reading:** 160 ms after the last key (two characters or more) and on Enter, `POST /api/sneak/read {id, text, picks}`; a later reply wins. The output shows, in order: the words with what was understood marked (`.sp-mark` buttons, "“…”, understood as …. Take it out": a click removes that stretch and reads again), then one card: a problem (`!ok`), a refusal (`#spRefused`, the reason and a button for `point_to`: Open Draft chase, Open ask creaite, the project page, Settings, Settings › People; cx-edit and the milestone form in words), a pick list (`#spChoices`: a click sends `picks` and reads again), quick facts (`#spFacts`, read only), go somewhere (`#spGo`), the chase hand-off (`#spChase`), a Claude-only draft (`#spClaude`: Draft it, or with AI drafts off `#spOff` with `needs_claude.why`), problems alone (`#spProblems`, amber), or the change card (`#spCard`: "Change <ID>", "New action from <ID>" or "Link <ID>"; "Understood in plain code" or "Read by Claude" from `by`; a from → to row per change, a derived priority marked "worked out"; the link or the new item ("ID given when applied"); `#spClose`, the optional closing update, when `offer_closing_update`; the problems left over; an optional "Word the update with Claude"; then the acts). Nothing "couldn't match" is said while typing, only after Enter.
- **People (`@`):** `@` before the caret (`atQuery`) opens `#spPeople` (`role="listbox"`, "People") under the output: up to six from the people register less hidden people, then anyone else who owns an item (`sneakPeople`), each with its organisation, never a VIP label (BL-288); filtered as you type (`atMatches`: the name, then a word of it, then an alias, by prefix). `#spIn` is the combobox (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `aria-activedescendant` on `spP<n>`); ↑ ↓ move (wrapping), Enter or Tab picks ("@Full Name ", the caret after it), a click picks without taking the focus, Esc closes the list only; a polite line says how many match; "No one in the people register matches …" when none does. While the list is open the words aren't read.
- **Making it:** Enter makes a plain change; the confirm list (`confirm` set: the card is amber, `#spNeed` "Needs a confirm: ⌘⏎ or click") takes ⌘⏎ (the page's ⌘⏎ list, `CMDENTER`, has the box first) or a click on `#spMake`; plain Enter only underlines the need. `sneakCommit` writes `stage.items` as one dashboard proposal with the peek's file name and envelope (`peek` meta: `k`, `id`, `label` = `stage.label`, note "Sneak peek"), adds it to `PKS`, applies at once (`applyNow({peek:true})`, BL-306), reconciles `PKS` and redraws the item. The changed fields (`stageFields`: the update's columns, a RAID priority with its impact or likelihood) get `.sp-flash` for 1.6 s, put back after a redraw (`sneakFlash`; the chip, the title or the free text in the peek, the editor's field in the milestone editor, Details otherwise), and again after Undo; it fades from `--warn-soft`, and under reduced motion holds still (the one `prefers-reduced-motion` rule and `data-motion="reduce"`). A closing update is sent back as `<words>, add an update: <text>` and read again first. The result line says what changed, with **Undo** (`/api/undo {apply_id}`; then "Undone. <ID> is back as it was.").
- **Claude's parts:** `needs_claude.request` goes to `/api/draft` through `draftAsk` (Stop while it runs); Yours beside Drafted, **Keep mine** or **Use this** (Enter); Use stages `{op:"update", changes:{<field>: text}, based_on}` as above, and is counted with `draftStat` (`used` or `rejected`). Kinds: `client` → `client_message`, `thinking` → `reasoning` or `options`, `sneak-update` (under Draft the latest update's switch, `DRAFTKIND`) → `latest_update`. `draftWhyNot` and `off` say why when AI drafts are off; the plain part still works.
- **Tasks:** `chase` closes the box and opens Draft chase with `{tone, to}` (`chaseOpen(start)`, `ChasePane`'s `start`); `goto` opens the source file (`/api/open`), the milestone (`openMilestone`), the project page (`go("cockpit")`) or ask creaite with the item attached ("About <ID> (<title>): …"). Nothing is sent from the box.
- **At 320 px:** the box keeps 8 px in, the compare columns and the change rows stack, and "Esc close" hides; nothing scrolls sideways (BL-217). No "VIP" anywhere (BL-288).

## New items in the peek (BL-179; sprint-peek-new)

Code: `system/templates/dashboard.html`, blocks marked `===== BL-179 (sprint-peek-new) =====` (CSS after BL-175's, JS at the end), and `newItemOf` (shared with `savePanel`).

- **Opening:** `openNew(k, how)` (the New tile, the Decisions menu, ⌘K's New action, New RAID item and New decision) sets `CUR={mode:"new", k, nid:"nw…", draft, blank, staged:null}` and draws the peek with the cursor in the title. `pkIs`, `pkCid` (the `nid`) and `pkX` (the draft) know the mode, so the chips, pickers, text, Details and rules are the peek's own. Defaults: the project from a project page, or Work filtered to exactly one project (`PROJF`), or the only active project, otherwise none ("Choose a project"); owner TBC ("Add owner"); status Open; priority `defaults.new_action_priority`; impact and likelihood Medium; RAID type from Work's Type filter when it names one, else Risk; Record a decision: Made, today, by Alan; Ask for a decision: Pending, no date, no maker. Withdrawn is disabled for a new decision.
- **What differs from an open item:** the ID line reads "New action" (or "New risk", "New decision") with the project as a chip (`[data-pkp="p"]`, Details' project list); a new decision has a **Source** line (`#pkSrcIn`, `data-pkf="srcIn"`), which the Create form also required; Copy link, More, Previous and Next are hidden; the feed says "Nothing here yet" until staged, then "You created this … · staged"; the "Last edited" line says "Not staged yet · it is staged once it has a title and a project" or "Staged · created when you apply".
- **The first commit:** `pkNewSync` runs on every change once the title is committed (and when the title is committed unchanged). It builds the form's fields from the draft (`FIELD[k]` plus `srcIn`), runs `validate(k,f,true)` and `norm`, and on success writes ONE `op:"new"` proposal with `writeProposal` and `newItemOf` (the Create form's route and values; never applied on the spot). A failed check shows the form's message in `#pkNote` and marks the field (`.pkbad`, `aria-invalid`), focusing it when nothing else has the cursor; nothing is staged. After staging, each change rewrites that proposal's `values` (`editProposal`); a change the check refuses is put back. `PKS` holds it as one entry (`isNew`, its title, project and the IDs known when staged), so the pill counts one change.
- **Apply:** ⌘↵ commits the field and, if the item can't be staged yet, stays open with the reason; otherwise the usual apply and close. After any apply `pkReconcile` (wrapped) finds the created item (same project, same title, an ID not known at staging) and, if its peek is still open, turns it into that record (`CUR.mode="record"`) without closing; the message names the new ID. **Undo last** on it removes the proposal and the peek goes back to the unstaged draft.
- **Leaving early:** `closePanel`, `curGuard` (another row, New again, `go`) and `restoreNav` (Back) are wrapped: an unstaged new item with a title shows `#pkAsk` (Keep, Discard; `EDITING` is on while it asks, so a click away waits; Esc on it returns to editing); with no title it closes and nothing is kept. Esc in one of its text fields keeps the typed text (not put back) and leaves the same way. Keep keeps the draft and closes; Discard drops it and closes; then the action that asked runs.
- **Reloads:** an unstaged new item is kept in `creaite-panel-drafts` under `new:<kind>` (BL-167's store and key), written 250ms after typing, on each change and on `pagehide`, with the fields that differ from the defaults; staging or closing it empty drops it. `openNew` offers it in `#pkDraft` (Restore or Discard; over 7 days old: dropped with a note). A draft left by the old Create form (dates as typed) is read too.
- **Dependency suggestions:** a RAID Dependency with no milestone (new or existing) shows the Create form's suggestions (`depSuggest`, "None needed" remembered by `depHash` of its text) under the key row; Attach is the milestone change.

## The Work and Review toolbars (BL-175; sprint-peek)

- The Keys chip is gone from both; the hint lines under the lists stay in the page, always hidden (the words' one source), and Help's keyboard table lists every key.
- Right-aligned icons: `#wfilters` and `#rvfilters` are `display:contents`, so their buttons are toolbar items and CSS `order` puts the funnel after the spacer: Work is search, field filters, space, funnel, List/Board, Display, New; Review is search, Kind, Needs, Source, space, funnel, Display, Approve routine. The DOM order is unchanged and matches what is seen, so Tab and the delegated handlers are untouched.
- Menus from the right-hand icons (the field picker, Display, Review's picker) open leftwards (`right:0`); any toolbar menu, and the saved views' menus, are kept inside the window when they open (`bl175Clamp`, a `translate` by the amount they would be cut off; on resize too).

## Weekend dates (BL-181; sprint-peek-new)

- **One rule:** `tracker.weekend(table, values)` (engine) and `weekendNote(iso)` (page) read `work.weekend_days` (names or numbers, Monday 0; Saturday and Sunday by default; `S.weekendDays` on the page). Fields: an action's and a RAID item's due date, a decision's needed-by and a Revisit written as a date. Never: raised, closed, made, a Quick capture note's date, milestones. A note, never a refusal.
- **Page:** `wkAsk(iso, anchor, apply)` shows `.wknote` ("Saturday 17-10-2026 is a weekend.", the Friday before and the Monday after as `[data-wkd]` buttons, Keep this date; `role="status"`), inline (the Board's dialog) or as a small layer (`.wkpop`, also `.cal` so the cell editor and the peek treat it as their own); the change waits for a choice. Gated: the peek's due and needed-by chips (`pkDate` wrapped; its typed box says so as it is typed), Revisit (`pkTextRule`), the table's date cell (`ceCommit`), the bulk bar (`bkApply`), an undated card's date (`undCommit`) and the Board prompt (`boardAsk`, after its past-date check). `dateHint` adds "a weekend" while a due date is typed. Both calendars mark weekend days (`.wkend`, ", weekend" in the name).
- **Review:** `revDetail` (wrapped) adds "Due on a Saturday" with the two days (`[data-wkrv]`), which set the proposal's `due_date` or `needed_by` with `editProposal` (`edited_in: dashboard`); `review.py list` prints the note; `propose.py`'s preview prints "Note: due on a Saturday (…); a working day is usually meant".
- **Early warning:** `weekend` (`warnings.weekend.on`, default on) in `build_dashboard.weekend_warnings`; Settings lists it (no threshold); the peek's warning line offers "Move to Friday …" and "Move to Monday …" (staged).

## The cockpit's metrics (BL-185; sprint-peek-new)

- The row under the status line: Overdue actions, Urgent risks, Milestone slip, Due Nd (and BL-51's Early warnings). "No due date" is no longer in it (FR-95's card is unchanged). "Due in 7 days" reads "Due 7d" and a slip "21d". A metric whose value is `.bad` has `ic("alert")` (`.evi`, `aria-hidden`) before its words. Every metric is one click target (`role="link"`): the Work lists as before, Milestone slip to the Milestones card (`data-msjump`), Early warnings to its card when there are any. Post-processed by a `renderCockpit` wrapper (`bl185Row`).

## Every count opens Work (BL-190; sprint-links)

- **The link:** `data-nav="<tab>:<view>:<code>[:<filters>]"`, read by `navParse`; the fourth part is `key=v1,v2&key2=…` (values URI-encoded) with `applyFilters`' keys, laid over the view's filters (`[]` clears one). An empty code is every project. `openWork(code, tab, view, extra)` is unchanged otherwise (it now merges `extra` onto the view, and an empty code sets no project filter). `countLink(label, n, nav, extra, inner, cls)` draws a figure: an `a.cntl` with `data-n` and `aria-label` "<what it counts>, open in Work", or plain text for zero. `jumpLink` opens the project page at a card (`data-cockjump="<code>|undated|ms"`) for a figure no one Work list holds. The Flow card's charts use `flkOpen` (an SVG `g.flk`, `role="link"`, `tabindex="0"`); an SVG with links is `role="group"`.
- **New filter:** **Closed between** (`cr`, `CR`), beside Due and Raised between: in the field picker, the chips, the address and saved views; an item with no closed date never matches.
- **Linked:** the Projects table's Evidence (Overdue, Urgent risks; No date and Slip open the project page's No due date and Milestones cards); the Flow card's opened and closed bars, ageing buckets (All items, Raised between) and priority segments, each only when the page's data gives the chart's number; the People list's Open actions, Open RAID, Decisions waiting and Overdue, and Nothing assigned's Closed items (the owner filter holds every spelling of the person on the items counted); a person's page (open actions, open risks and issues, overdue, decisions waiting); Today's "n of your actions are overdue" and Your commitments' count; the project page's Dependencies and Decisions needed counts; the prep brief's Open actions (Overdue and due-soon actions for the portfolio), Risks and issues with no response and Decisions needed; Settings › People's Actions and RAID (any status).
- **Open only (Alan, 11-10-2026):** Actions' All open, Mine, Due soon and Chase list status Open; Delayed has its own view. A figure that says "open" and opens one of those views counts status Open only (`openOnly`): the project page's "All n open actions", the People list's and a person's page's open actions, the prep brief's Open actions for a project. A figure by date counts Open and Delayed (`overdue`, `soon`, `undatedWork`) and its link carries `st=Open,Delayed`: the Overdue view does already; the project page's "Due in n days" box, Today's Your commitments, the cockpit's Actions due or overdue (`dueor`), the prep brief's portfolio figure and the No due date links add it. The person page's Open work list, Hand over and "See all actions in Work" keep Open and Delayed (they list the work, with the status filter shown). All items keeps its own buckets (Open holds Delayed and Pending), so its figures are unchanged.
- **Plain, and why:** the Flow card's overdue-by-week bars (rebuilt from the history of due dates); the prep brief's Decisions since then (no date range for decisions), Decisions to revisit, Early warnings and Recent reports; the project page's Client messaging count and "n at risk" dependencies; Settings › Workstreams' Open (actions and RAID together, with no workstream filter on All items); Review and Activity (proposals and changes, not work items).
- **Look:** the figure keeps its colour (red stays red), a dotted underline at rest, solid with a tint on hover, the focus ring on the keyboard; a chart bar gains an outline. A click on a figure never also opens the row, card or dialog it sits in (the prep dialog closes).

## Milestones: Plan and Movement (BL-186, BL-184; sprint-milestones)

The spec is `dev/mockups/milestones-views-mockup.html` (v6); the page follows it, on the real data. CSS and JS in `===== BL-186 (sprint-milestones) =====` blocks (classes `mv-`), the slip range in `===== BL-184 … =====`.
- **Data:** each milestone in `projects[].ms` carries index 14 from `build_dashboard.milestone_detail`: `st` (the state, from `tracker.milestone_state`), `sug` (the dates' suggestion), `set`, `why`, `owner`, `actual`, `client`, `cw`, `cword`, `cvis` (the client filter, `tracker.client_milestones`), `orig` (the first agreed date in `change_log`), `agr`, `fc`, `pc`, `moves`, `rb` (the last rebaseline and its decision), `hist` (forecast and agreed at each reporting Friday), `log` (What changed), `note` (the internal note) and `range` (`forecast.py`). The page computes nothing it shows a client from its own reading: the client view uses `cvis` and `cword`.
- **The page:** `#v-milestones`, `go("milestones", CODE)`; address `#milestones=CODE&v=plan|movement[&ms=ID]` (`navState`, `hashOf`, `parseHash` and `restoreNav` wrapped; `HASH0` keeps an address opened directly until the block has run). The rail keeps Projects; the tab reads "creaite · <Project> milestones". `mvOpenPage(code, view, id, {add})` is the one opener: `openMilestone` (search, citations, ⌘K, Activity, the Dependencies card) and `openNewMilestone` (⌘K's Add milestone) call it.
- **One Plan:** `mvPlan(code, cli, host)` draws the line and the rows for the cockpit card (`host` "card", compact) and the page ("page"). Labels are placed on levels so none overlap (`mvStrip`); a later forecast is a dashed ring with a dated label joined to the agreed date; the line scrolls (‹ › Today) and remembers where it was. A dependency with a date warning marks the row it blocks ("waiting on …", internal only; BL-90).
- **Views and marks:** `MVAUD` ("int" or "cli", `creaite.cockpit.milestones.view`) and `MVDET` (`creaite.cockpit.milestones.details`) are kept in this browser, and the page works without storage. The card's switch (`#mvSwap`, `aria-pressed`, its label and tooltip saying what it switches to) flips the card and the page only. The marks are BL-244's (sprint-milestone-ui, below): only the Internal view is marked. Movement is `aria-disabled` in Client view; switching to Client on Movement returns to Plan; Rebaseline… and the slip metric switch back to Internal with a message.
- **The editor:** `#mvPeek`, fixed at the peek's size (half the window, never under 520px; with ask creaite it stops at the chat or the chat sits over it with its content padded clear, `--pkover`). Each change is `mvSave` → `save()` (one dashboard proposal; with auto-apply applied at once and offered back with Undo), queued (`MVQ`) so each is written after the last is applied. Dates come from `#mvCal` (no typed box: BL-186), people from `#mvPop` (search, Enter, Esc; a new person through the register's own dialog, `ownerFinal`). A click outside closes it (`MVJUST` ignores the opening click; another milestone switches; `MVPOPWAS` lets the first click close only an open calendar or list; a pending state or forecast reason shakes it, not under reduced motion). A refresh redraws the page and the editor only when their milestones changed (`mvSig`).
- **Decide what to do** stages pending proposals for Review (`mvStage`): Hold (a Made decision), Rebaseline (the milestone's change with its `rebaseline` block, approved on its own in Review), Add recovery action (an action linked to the milestone, owner TBC), Escalate to steering (a Pending decision).
- **The slip range** (`mvRangeLine`) sits in Movement's detail only.

## Sprint milestone-ui: the two looks, Add milestone in one step, the history feed (BL-244, BL-245, BL-250 A; sprint-milestone-ui)

CSS and JS in `===== BL-244 (sprint-milestone-ui) =====`, `===== BL-245 … =====` and `===== BL-250 A … =====` blocks.
- **Client plain, internal marked (BL-244).** `mvCardCls(cli)`: the Client view is `mv-card card client`, the cockpit's own card (its theme's edge and background, no shadow), with no pill (`mvPill(true)` is empty), no `.mv-hidenote` and no banner. The Internal view is `mv-card internal`: a 2px `--warn` border with a 3px `--warn-soft` halo, and `.mv-pill.int` filled `--warn` with `--surface` words and the eye-off icon (never colour alone). Both keep `print-color-adjust: exact`; in print the halo goes and the pill keeps a `--warn` border, so the words never print white on white. `--warn` against `--surface` meets 3:1 for the border, and `--surface` on `--warn` 4.5:1 for the pill, in all nine themes, light and dark. Same on the card, the Plan and Movement. **First visit:** `MVAUD` is Client when nothing is stored ("client view (default)", Alan); a stored choice is kept. What the Client view hides (`mvVis`) is unchanged.
- **Add milestone in one step (BL-245).** `mvEdOpen(null, from, {add:true})` (the page's **+ Add milestone**, the card's empty-state button, ⌘K's "Add milestone") sets `MV.add` to `mvDraftNew()` and `mvAddDraw` shows every field at once: name, State (the five, following the dates until one is picked; a state better than the dates asks "Why …?", Achieved asks "Achieved on", today by default), Agreed and Forecast (calendar picks; the first agreed date fills the forecast), Progress (slider and %), Description, Owner (the people list), Visible to client with its wording; no summary, no Rebaseline (a first agreed date is a pick). Nothing is saved until **Create milestone** (`mvAddCommit`) or Enter in the name: it checks the editor's rules (a name; a reason for a better state; an achieved date not after today; progress 0 to 100; client wording when visible), writes one `new` proposal with every field through `save()`, so `tracker.py` checks it again, auto-apply applies it and the message offers Undo; then the editor becomes the saved milestone's. A save that fails keeps the draft. With something entered, an outside click or another milestone shakes instead of closing; Esc or Cancel discards. Editing an existing milestone is unchanged.
- **The history feed (BL-250 part A).** The peek's feed is drawn by shared helpers: `feedLines(lines, typeOf, textOf)` (an item's history from `pkHistory`, ACT plus the older history `D.activity_older` of BL-250 B, as entries), `feedTabsHtml` and `feedListHtml`; `pkFeedItems` and `pkFeed` use them. `feedBlock(host, items)` draws a feed with its own tabs (`data-fdtab`, `FDTAB[host]`, arrow keys) for `host` "ms" (the milestone editor's **History** section) and "proj" (a full-width row under the project panel's fields, `openProject`). It draws every entry once (each with `data-t`); a tab only sets the section's `data-tab`, which CSS filters by, and the tabs' selection, so no HTML is written for a tab. The milestone feed is drawn again with its editor (after each save); the project panel's when it opens. Milestone: Activity is each field change, Updates the reason given with a change (the apply's `note`, shown "reason: …"), Source its creation and its `source` (`milestone_detail`'s `src`); not shown in Client view (`#mvHistHid` says so). Project: Activity is health, its reason and the other fields, Updates the Current state edits (`file_activity`) and the weekly `updates` rows ("weekly update, week ending DD-MM-YYYY: …"), Source its creation; `projHistory` gathers the project's and its weekly rows' lines from both, newest first. `mCreated` falls back to the older history's "created" line.

## Sprint polish-themes (BL-192, BL-191, BL-40a to BL-40d, the colour audit; sprint-polish-themes)

- **Settings is a gear (BL-192).** `RAIL_ITEMS`' Settings uses `ic("gear")` (Lucide's settings shape, added to `ICONS`); Activity's "Setting" type uses it too. The old `settings` shape stays in `ICONS` (nothing else draws it).
- **The rail is customised by right-click (BL-191).** `renderRail()` no longer makes `#railCust`; the foot group is the foot pages and the "…" button. A `contextmenu` listener on the document opens the BL-163 popover (`railPopOpen(opener, y)`) for any right-click inside `.rail` and prevents the browser's menu; anywhere else the browser's menu is left alone. The opener (focus goes back to it on Esc or close) is the rail button right-clicked, else the button that had focus (the context-menu key, Shift+F10, sends a `contextmenu` with no pointer position), else the current page's button. The popover sits beside the rail level with the click, kept in the window. `.rail` has `aria-describedby="railHint"` (a visually hidden line that says so). Settings › General › Appearance › Left rail: **Customise…** (`#railCustSet`) is unchanged, and ⌘K has **Customise the rail**.
- **Nine themes (BL-40a to BL-40d).** `THEMES` adds Claude, Apple, Retro Arcade and Thermo Scan (ids as shown, the space included: `data-palette="Retro Arcade"`). Each has the three token blocks and shape rules scoped to its palette, after Cursor's. Beyond the tokens every theme sets, the four set `--btn`, `--btn-ink`, `--btn-hover` (the primary button, and the Milestones page's and the peek's primary buttons), `--bad-ink`, `--stage-dot`, `--line-strong`, `--shadow-card` and `--shadow-pop`; Apple sets `--display`; Retro Arcade `--pixel`, `--arcade-logo`, `--arcade-red`; Thermo Scan `--spectrum` and `--spectrum-x`. The colours, what was kept and what was changed for contrast are in BUILD › Sprint polish-themes.
- **Colour tokens (the audit).** Every colour is a token. The base `:root` adds `--bad-ink` (#FFFFFF: text on the rail's count badge and the hidden-pages count), `--knob` (#FFFFFF: the peek switch's knob) and `--stage-dot` (#F0C66B: the peek's staged dot), with the old literal values, so the five older themes are unchanged. Health, priority, status, due and weekend colours are `--ok`, `--warn`, `--bad` (with their `-soft` grounds) and `--accent`; text `--ink`, `--muted`, `--faint`; borders `--line` (and `--line-strong` where a theme has one). Fixed on purpose: shadows and scrims, `#dePrev` and `DE_COLOURS` (the Word preview), the image viewer's white backing, Help's `--hp-*`.

## Sprint review-work (BL-200, BL-198, BL-197; sprint-review-work)

- **Change the project in Review (BL-200).** `revRow` is wrapped: each undecided action, RAID item or decision proposal, and each set of minutes, gets a **Project** button in `.rowact` (`data-act="pj"`, `.rvpj`, `aria-haspopup="menu"`); an update proposal has none. A capture-phase click handler opens `rvMoveMenu` (a `pkList` menu of the active projects, the current one disabled with "Its project now") or, on minutes, `rvMoveMinutes`: when `r.projects` has several, a first menu asks whose items to move (the row's group first, each with its item count); then the target menu; then `rvMovePreview`, a `.pkpop.rvmv` dialog from `POST /api/review/assign` with `dry_run` (headline "Move 9 items from X to Y", the files line, the update items that stay, and, when items were moved on their own before, a **Leave those** / **Move all** choice that re-runs the preview). **Move** posts it for real; the snack has Undo (moves them back, `moved: "all"`; none when the target already had the minutes). **BL-281:** `review.py move_item` keeps what a move clears (milestone, workstream, the new-workstream suggestion) in the item's `moved_cleared`, keyed by the project it left, and a move back to that project (Undo, item or minutes, or by hand) restores each into an empty field. An item move posts `/api/review/assign {file, ref, code}`; its snack offers **Remember <signal>** (BL-28) when the item has a learnable signal, else Undo (move back). Without the live server an item is moved in the proposal by the page (`editProposal`, the same fields as `review.py`); minutes say they need the server. In the peek, `pkDrawProps` is wrapped: a proposal gets a **Project** chip (`data-pkp="p"`, the Details picker `PKPROP.p`) after Owner and Due, held in the draft until Save as the other fields are. `savePanel` and `rvSet` record `moved_from` and clear a workstream the new project hasn't. `build_dashboard.review_queue` adds `mf` (moved_from) and `op` to each item row.
- **The peek follows a click (BL-198).** `hlRowClick` is wrapped (Work's table and Board, a person's page): with the peek open (`pkOn()`), a plain left click that leaves exactly that row highlighted also runs `openRecord(k, id)`; Review's click handler calls `pkFollowRev(e, n)`, which opens `openProposal(n)` for an action, RAID or decision proposal. Not followed: a Ctrl/⌘, Shift or Alt click, a click on the ID (it opened already), a link, a button, a tick box or a cell editor, the row already shown, and on Review minutes, reports, file changes and updates (highlight only), and a proposal with unsaved changes (`PK.dirty`: `pkNote` says save or cancel first). `pkKeepFocus` puts focus back where it was (or blurs the panel's own focus), so the keys stay on the list.
- **The right-click menu (BL-197).** One component: `cxOpen(items, x, y, {label, parent, search})` builds `.pkpop.cxmenu` with `role="menu"` and `role="menuitem"` buttons (icon, word, an optional hint, a tick for the current value, a chevron for a submenu; `.cxhead` headings and `.cxsep` separators); submenus open beside their item on the side that fits (`cxPlace`, BL-175's rule), and a `search` submenu has the people search box. Keys (window capture, so the lists' own keys never see them): ↑ ↓ Home End, → or Enter opens a submenu, ← or Esc closes it (Esc on the top menu closes it and returns focus), Enter and Space run, Tab closes. A click outside, a wheel, a resize or leaving the window closes it. `cxTarget(el)` decides: a Review row (`#reviewBody tr[data-n]`, undecided), a `[data-row]` on Work, a person's page or the project page (the kind from `data-k`, the tab, or `kOfId`), or a `[data-open="kind:id"]` link (Today, Activity, the project page's cards); never inputs, text being edited, real links, the rail (BL-191 keeps it), the peek, the chat or a dialog, where the browser's menu stays. Shift+F10 and the ContextMenu key open it on the focused item or the list's cursor row (`cxCursor`); the `contextmenu` the key also fires is swallowed for 400 ms. Lists: `cxWork` (actions, RAID: Open, Close or Reopen, Status, Owner, Due, Priority or Impact and Likelihood, Workstream, Milestone, Copy ID, Copy link, Draft email to the owner; decisions: Open, Record the decision, Withdraw, Needed by, Decision maker, the copies), saved with `boardMove`; `cxWorkMany` for several highlighted (same kind: Close, Reopen, Status, Owner, Due, Priority or Impact and Likelihood, Workstream and Milestone only in one project; decisions: Decision maker; mixed kinds: Copy IDs only), saved with `batchMove`; `cxReview` (Approve, Reject, Open, then the proposal's Project (`rvMoveItem`), Owner, Due, Priority or Impact and Likelihood, Workstream, Milestone, Status through `rvSet`; minutes: Approve, Reject, Open the draft, Project) and `cxReviewMany` (Approve n through `rvApproveSelected`, so the bulk rule holds; Reject n; the shared fields). Right-clicking a row outside the highlight highlights it alone first. `Due ›` offers Today, Tomorrow, Friday, Next week (Monday), Pick a date… (`boardAsk`) and Clear, each with `weekendNote`'s text when it falls on a weekend.

## AI drafts (BL-219, BL-222, BL-225, BL-226; sprint-drafts-fields)

- **The scaffold** (`/* ===== AI drafts: the scaffold … */`, used by every draft kind and by the drafts-extraction and drafts-writing sprints): `DRAFTFEATURES` (the Settings switches, `drafting.<feature>`, on when missing; `S.drafting` from `ui_settings`), `DRAFTKIND` (kind → feature), `draftBtn(kind, word, tip, attrs)` (`.drbtn`: the spark and a word; greyed with `aria-disabled` and a tooltip saying why when off, without the live server or while a draft runs), `draftAsk(body, {label, status})` (POST `/api/draft`; the run badge, progress and **Stop** through `status(html)`; resolves to the parsed draft, `{nothing}`, `{stopped}` or `{message}`), `draftCardHtml(d, {title, use, extra, from})` (`.drcard`: the text with each sentence's `S1` tags, `mark.drloose` and a **no source** tag for one without; Generate's `.gensrc` details "Drafted from n sources" with **Open** for a file; Discard and Use), `draftStat(feature, event)`.
- **The peek's host** (BL-219's block, shared): `PKDRBTN` (functions returning `{f, kind, word, tip}` for the item and mode), `PKDRBODY`, `PKDRTITLE`, `PKDREXTRA`. The button sits in `#pkDescL` for the free text (`upd`, `resp`, `why`) and on its own line (`.drline`) under a Details field (`opts`, `cm`, `cmd`). One card at a time (`PKDRC`), in `#pkAiDraft` under the free text or `#pkAiDraftD` under Details; leaving the item drops it (counted rejected). **Use** focuses the field, puts the text in with the cursor at its end and a note ("not saved yet"); leaving the field stages it through `pkText` (counted used or edited by comparing with the draft), **Esc** puts the old text back (counted rejected).
- **BL-219:** Draft beside an open action's Latest update (record mode). **Draft updates** on `#wbulk` when every highlighted row is an action, and on Today's `#drStale` line under Your commitments (open actions stale by `review.stale_after_days`); both call `draftBulk(ids)`, then reload and offer **Open Review**. Review rows from a draft carry `.drrv` ("Drafted"), and `setDecision` counts an approval or rejection.
- **BL-225:** Draft under Client messaging in Details while client-visible is on (or waiting for messaging); the card's extra lists **Taken out** and **Check before you use it**. The project page's Client messaging card gets **Draft all** (`data-drcmall`).
- **BL-222:** Suggest beside a RAID item's response (labelled by `RESPL`), a decision's Reasoning and Options considered, in record and proposal mode (a proposal sends `{proposal: {file, ref}, edits}`; a new risk with no response says **Draft a response**); the card says "Suggested from"; a risk's card has **Set impact to …** and **Set likelihood to …** (`pkImpLik`, a staged change), or "…, as it is now". Review rows for a new risk with no response have **Draft a response** (`data-drrisk`), which opens the proposal and asks.
- **BL-226:** `#hDraft` under the health form's evidence (`healthFacts(code)`: `healthEvidence(code).reasons`, then the project's early warnings), Use fills `#f_reason`; `#mvFcDraft` under the milestone editor's "Why is the forecast moving…?", Use fills `#mvFcWhy` and `MV.pend.why`. Movement's slip range line adds `.mv-explain` with `range.explain` (forecast.py's template).
- **Settings › General › AI drafts:** a switch per feature (`data-drsw`) and, from `/api/drafts`, each feature's counts.

## AI drafts: writing (BL-223, BL-224, BL-227; sprint-drafts-writing)

- **One HTML setter.** `putHtml(el, html)` is the one place this sprint's blocks set HTML (BL-228 D's direction); the Prep popup's body and status, Generate's status and the Draft email's body, preview and note use it too, so the page's HTML-sink count went down.
- **BL-223, Draft agenda.** In the Prep popup's bar (`prepPageBar`): `agendaBtn()` (`draftBtn("agenda", …)`), the **POAD** and **Lighter** chips (`data-agstyle`, remembered in this browser as `creaite-agenda-style`; the default is `S.agendaStyle`, from settings `drafting.agenda_style`), and the host `#prepAgenda` (kept across redraws in `AGD`). The meeting page's Next step card adds **Draft agenda** beside Make the prep page (`mhAct("agenda")` opens the prep and asks). The card (`agendaCardHtml`): Purpose, Objectives, Approach, Deliverables (or one paragraph) with each sentence's sources, the timed agenda (`ol.aglist`: time, point, minutes), "Drafted from…", **Save it for attendees** (otherwise Internal), Discard, **Save to Agendas** (`/api/agenda/save`), **Copy as plain text** and **Copy** (`agendaCopy(d)`: Outlook-safe inline styles as the Draft email, with plain text alongside). Copy or Save counts `used`; Discard, before either, `rejected`.
- **BL-224, the Draft email's modes.** `draftEmail(o)` dispatches on `o.mode`: `update` (BL-83, the same HTML and text as before; `o.cover` replaces the opening paragraph), `chase` (`deChase`: `DE_CHASE[level]`, level 0–2 from `deChaseLevel(items, chased)` over the ticked items; "Chased 03-10-2026, once" under each row) and `followup` (`deFollowup`: thanks with the meeting and its weekday date, Summary, Decisions, Actions grouped by owner in `deOwners` order: `o.lead`, then the attendees in order, then the rest; To is the attendees' addresses). The row, table and plain-text lines are shared (`deRowHtml`, `deTableHtml`, `deTextRows`).
