The popup: `openDraftEmail(name, mode)` (mode chips **Update request** and **Chase overdue items**, the latter off when nothing of theirs is overdue or it is Alan's own page), `openFollowup(token)` from the meeting page's **Draft the follow-up email** (`data-defollow`; only items in the tracker; `/api/email/followup` fills the attendees and the summary, `deOutsideAll`), **Lead with** in a follow-up, **I sent it** in a chase (`/api/chases`; `SA().chases` updates the person page's `#ppChased` line), and **Polish** with Warm, Neutral or Firm (`draftAsk({kind:"polish", text, tone})`; `#dePolish` shows yours beside the polished one: **Keep mine** or **Use this**). The right-click menu adds **Chase <owner>** on an overdue item owned by someone else.
- **BL-227, Generate's Form.** `#genFormKind` (`GEN_FORMS`: Current state, Steering note, Three-line update, Email paragraph, Teams message; remembered as `creaite-gen-form`); `GEN_AUD` gains Steering committee and Sponsor (`GEN_AUDNOTE`). A form other than Current state disables Draw on (and Length for the three fixed-length forms), relabels the button **Write it** and keeps the dialog open: `genFormDraft` asks `draftAsk({kind:"status-form", …})` and shows `genFormCard` in `#genOut` (each sentence with its sources, bold and bullets shown, a line per sentence for the steering note and three-line update, the length check, "Written from…", Discard, **Copy as plain text**, **Copy** except for a Teams message). Nothing is saved; closing the dialog without copying counts `rejected`.

## Extraction suggestions and Quality (BL-221, BL-218, BL-220, BL-233; sprint-drafts-extraction, v1.7.0)

- **BL-221 Looks like** (`/* ===== BL-221 (sprint-drafts-extraction) … */`): a Review row's `dup` comes from the staged `looks_like` mark (build_dashboard's `repeat_mark`: `{id, t, state, same, project, strength, staged}`), else BL-133's hint worded the same way. The row's tag (`llTag`) says "Looks like ABC-A-009 (same project, open) · Very likely" (another project's name, or closed, where that's where it is; "Very likely" or "Possible", never a percentage). The proposal's peek and panel note (`llNoteHtml`) add **Compare** (`#llDlg`: the two side by side, built with DOM nodes, a differing value marked "· differs" as well as tinted), **Merge as an update to …** (staged marks only, same project and open), **Keep as new** (staged marks only) and **Reject as duplicate of …** (BL-133's `data-rvdup`). Merge and Keep go through `/api/review/repeat` (review.py merge / keep-new) and need the live server; without it the snack gives the Claude Code words.
- **BL-297 D, Update an existing item instead** (`/* ===== BL-297 D (v19-review-picker) … */` in `src/review.js`): offered on a pending row with `op === "new"`, a project and kind action, RAID item or decision (`rtOK`). Two places: the proposal's peek (`rtNoteHtml` in `#pkPropTop`, after the Looks like note: a `.mini` button, `data-rvrt`, `aria-haspopup="listbox"`) and the row's right-click menu (`cxReview`: "Update an existing item instead…", anchored on the row's Edit button). Not on the row itself, so the default Review screen is unchanged. `rtOpen` asks `/api/review/candidates` `{file, ref}` (the live server; without it a message names the Claude Code command) and opens `pkList` as a listbox (search when over seven; ↑ ↓ Home End, Enter, Esc and Tab close, focus back to the button): each option is "ID · title" with a hint of "Looks like" for the marked item, the strength and why ("Very likely: Same thread · Dana"; never a percentage), the owner and "Due DD-MM-YYYY". No items: a message ("No open action in … for it to update"), no picker. `rtGo` posts `/api/review/retarget` `{file, ref, id}`, closes the peek if it shows that proposal, reloads the data and says "Now an update to ID" (or "Rejected as a duplicate of ID: it carried nothing new") with **Undo** (⌘Z too): `{file, ref, undo: true}` (`review.py unmerge`), then reloads. A refusal is the server's message. No "VIP" wording anywhere (BL-288).
- **BL-218 Looks done:** a probably-done update's row (`r.dn`: strength, evidence line, reason) leads its detail line with `dnHtml`: a tag (**Looks done** in the ok colour, **Maybe done** plain, **Not done** in the warn colour; the word is always there) and the evidence ("Mail 09-10-2026 15:02 by Alan Carter: “attached the SOW”"). It is a closing update: individual approval, never in Approve routine. An open promise that looks kept (BL-125 A, decision 10) shows the same way, its update setting status Done. `setDecision` counts it (`done`: used, edited when `as_proposed` is there, rejected) and a repeat rejected as its duplicate (`repeat` used).
- **BL-220 Sharpen on capture:** `capChips(text, code, have)` (plain code: `chOwner` from `personPool()` or "I'll…" as `S.me`, `chDue` through `parseWhen` after by/on/before/due/until, workstream and milestone named in the words or from `chSimilar`, the closest past action of the project, and priority words) gives `{f, v, label, why}` for fields not yet set; `chipEls` draws them as buttons (DOM, `.capchip`). Three places: `#pkChips` under the peek's title for a new action (a click is `pkCommit` of that one field), `ckChipsHtml` in ⌘K's add-an-action preview (a click sets the field on `CK[CKI].change.new` and redraws), and `#qcChips` under Quick capture's notes for the line at the cursor (a click adds "(owner …)" to the line). `vagueWhy(title)` (under four words or no verb, as `extraction.vague`) shows **Sharpen** (`PKDRBTN`, `f: "t"`, placed in `#pkChips` and hidden while the title is clear) for a new action or a proposal; the card ("Sharpened action") shows **As written:** and the why; Use puts the text in the title, unsaved, and leaving it stages it. Review rows of a vague new action (`inferred` description) get **Sharpen** (`data-shrv`: opens the proposal and asks). ⌘K says when the typed title is vague.
- **BL-233 Activity › Quality:** Activity has two tabs (`.acttabs`, arrow keys move between them): **Changes** (the list, the bar, What ask creaite changed) and **Quality** (`#actQuality`, `renderQuality()` from `D.quality`, built with DOM nodes): the period, one line on what moved, By source and By type tables (approved as proposed, approved after an edit, rejected, each "n of decided"; waiting), the reasons, the duplicates the tracker recorded, the fields edited most with where, "field unknown" for edits before BL-232, and the AI drafts (offered; used, edited, rejected out of offered). A group under 10 has "Not rated yet: n decided, rated from 10" and no percentage; a feature never offered says "Not offered yet".
- **Settings › General › AI drafts** lists three more switches: Spot repeats of existing items (`repeat`), Spot items that are probably done (`done`) and Sharpen a vague action (`sharpen`).

## Sprint insight (BL-207, BL-209, BL-205, BL-206, BL-208; sprint-insight)

- **Evidence on hover (BL-207).** A drafted Current state (Generate, Make it executive) arrives with each sentence ending in `{F3, F7}`, the facts sheet's IDs (`status_facts.py`, `fid`). `claimsWrap` takes the marks out and wraps each sentence in `span.claim[data-c]`: a dotted underline with a source, a dashed one and "No source ·" without (never colour alone), none once edited by hand. Click (or, in the executive version and the read view, focus) opens `#evPanel` in the peek's place, clear of the chat: the sentence, its state, each fact with its rows (status, owner, dates; each opens its item) and the quoted line with its file and line (read from the file by `/api/current/check` or `/api/current/sources`, never stored), and "Every claim in this paragraph has a source" or how many do. It is marked Internal. Tab and ⇧Tab move between claims while it is open; Esc closes it. Save waits on a sentence creaite wrote with no source until it is edited or kept with a note (Keep it, Keep all). On Save, `/api/current/evidence` writes `system/staging/evidence/<code>-current.json` (sentence hashes to facts and source IDs; never the sentence); the read view and a reopened editor find saved sentences by hash, and the card says how many were changed since. **Check sources** wraps every unlinked sentence and re-checks it against today's facts (plain code: the same date or number and words).
- **The meeting hub (BL-209).** `#meeting=<token>` (a hash of the meeting key: `calendar:<event>`, `fp:…` for a quick note or a file with no event, `cal:<day>|<start>|<title>` without an ID). The strip: Invite, Prep page, Transcript, Draft minutes, Coverage, Review, Follow-up, each done (✓), now (●, `aria-current="step"`), to do (○) or not needed (–); before the meeting only its prep can be "now". Then Next step (one button: Make the prep page, Load the transcript, Process the transcript, Review the minutes and n items, See the actions due), What it produced (yours first), Coverage with **Carry to the next agenda**, Promised to you, Files. Linked from Today's day view and Week ahead, Intake's Meetings (the time cell), the prep brief, Files, an item's source and Review's minutes row. All of it is in the page's data, so it shows without the live server; Carry needs it.
- **⌘K changes (BL-205).** Typed text is read by `ckParse` (plain code) as a change: move or set a date (`parseWhen`; `+2w` from the item's own date), mark done or close, reopen, set the owner (people register: name, alias, email), the priority or the status, add an action, risk or decision (`add action for ABC: text, owner, when`). It is listed first under **Change**, with the preview `#ckPrev` under the list: the item, each field − before and + after, and what else it touches (items tied to the milestone, the baseline slip, a milestone it would pass, the next meeting for the project, a weekend date, an unknown owner). Two matching items or people show under "Change · which one?"; Enter picks and the palette stays open. Enter stages (the peek's staging: never applied, BL-189 holds), ⌘Enter stages and opens Review, Esc changes nothing. A request it can't read but that starts with a verb is offered as "Give it to ask creaite". Nothing deletes; a phrase that is a palette entry ("New action") or has fewer than three words stays a jump.
- **Close the day (BL-206).** Today's `#dayClose` line: **Close the day** (or "Look at the day again"), "Closed yesterday at 18:10", and the nudge after `day.close_from` (Settings › Essentials › Your day; 17:30 by default since BL-320, empty for none). The card `#closeDlg`: Moved today, You promised today (Add it to your commitments approves the pending proposal), Still open, the next working day's meetings with their prep pages (Make the missing prep page opens the prep brief), and on a Friday the weekly report line. **Close the day** posts `/api/day/close`, which writes only `system/staging/day-closed.json`. Also in ⌘K.
- **Close the day, a guided shutdown (BL-295, v1.9.0; replaces BL-206's one card).** `components/closeday/CloseDay.jsx` (Preact, on the `Dialog` building block: native `<dialog>` with `showModal`, named by `#dcH`, Esc asks the owner, focus back to the opener). `openClose()` (the door, ⌘K) calls `openCloseDay()`, which mounts it once into `#closeDayHost`; its step, choices and catch-up state live there while the page is open. Four steps in `ol.cdsteps` (`li.done`, `li.now` with `aria-current="step"`), the body a `role=group` named "Step n of 4, <name>" and a polite live region saying the same; Back and Next (`[data-cdback]`, `[data-cdnext]`) or ← → outside a field; Esc is Not yet and keeps the step. A step with nothing in it (Catch up off or unavailable; nothing moved or promised; no loose ends and no sweep running) is passed over both ways and shown done; Tomorrow always shows.
  1. **Catch up:** on opening, `/api/day/card` then `/api/day/catchup`. Start or busy: `.cdsweep[aria-busy]` with the source being read (`ol.cdsrc`: Mail, Teams chats, Teams channels, Matching to projects, from the run's steps through `/api/ask/attach`), **Stop** (`[data-cdstop]`, `/api/ask/stop`) and **Skip** (`[data-cdskip]`, `/api/day/catchup/skip`); `/api/day/catchup/status` is polled every 1.5 s while it runs or waits (and, hidden, while a close waits for it). Done: "Caught up at HH:MM." with up to five finds (type, words, project) and "and n more"; the card is read again so Loose ends include them. Recent: the line with a quiet **Sweep again**; offer (`day.catchup_sweep` "offer"): "Catch up before you close?" with **Sweep now** (`[data-cdsweep]`, `force: true`); stopped, skipped or failed: one line and Sweep now.
  2. **Today:** Moved today and You promised today (Add it to your commitments, as BL-206).
  3. **Loose ends:** `/api/day/card`'s `loose` (the catch-up's finds first, tagged New; other proposals; Intake; actions and RAID items due today, then overdue with their date). Each row (`li[data-ref]`) has a `role=group` of three `aria-pressed` buttons (`[data-place]`: Review now, First thing tomorrow (default), Leave); `.cdall` sets them all; the first eight, then **and n more** (`[data-cdmore]`). Nothing is approved here.
  4. **Tomorrow:** the next working day's meetings (a private one left out) with Prep page made, being made, or **Make prep page** (`[data-cdprep]`, `aria-pressed`, queued for after closing); on a Friday the weekly report line; the First thing tomorrow list (five, then "and n more").
  **Close the day** (`[data-dcdone]`) posts `/api/day/close {choices, prep}`. Closed: the end line `.cddone` "Day closed at HH:MM" and "Everything has a place: n for Review now, n first thing tomorrow, n prep pages being made", **Look at the day again** (`[data-cdagain]`) and **Review now** or **Done** (`[data-cdfinish]`). Review now (`cdOpenPlaces`) opens Review with the chosen proposals highlighted (`RHL`), else Work filtered to the chosen items' project and kind and highlighted (`HL`), else Intake, with a message offering the next place; nothing is staged. `pending: true` (the catch-up still running): "Closing when the sweep finishes" (Hide), then the end line when it ends; its unplaced finds go to First thing tomorrow (server). The door's label and colour follow `D.day.closed`.
- **First thing (BL-295).** Today's brief opens with `#ftSec` (hidden when empty): `D.day.first.items` still `open`, from the last Close the day, "from DD-MM-YYYY" in `#nFt` (`components/closeday/FirstThing.jsx`, `mountFirstThing`). A tracker item opens its peek (`data-open`), a proposal opens Review highlighted, a file or transcript opens Intake. `session.py start` prints the same list after the reminders.
- **Close the day's settings (BL-295, placed by BL-320):** Catch up before closing (`day.catchup_sweep`: Start it when the card opens, Ask first, Off) is in Essentials › Your day; Catch up when the last sweep is older than (minutes) (`day.catchup_after_minutes`, 30) is Advanced › Close the day.
- **One at a time (BL-208).** Review's toolbar button **One at a time** opens `#rvFocus` over the list: "n of m", the card (kind, Project control, title, fields with guessed ones dashed and "Guessed: why", the source beside it), A Approve, R Reject (the reason menu), E Edit, J Skip, K Previous, Esc back to the list. The queue is `D.review_focus` (`review.focus_queue`): guessed fields first, then what needs you, then the spot-checks (one routine item in `review.spot_check_every`, 10; random but stable per batch; never zero in the first month); the routine rest is approved together at the end by the list's rule. Where you stopped is kept per batch.
## UI audit fixes (BL-211 to BL-215; sprint-ui-audit, 1.6.0)

- **ask creaite gives focus back (BL-211):** closing the chat (Esc, ×, ⌘J) with focus in it returns focus to what had it when the chat opened: the rail button, the row (found again by its key if the list was redrawn), the field, or the ask creaite button when it was clicked. If that is gone or hidden, the page's heading or its rail button; never `<body>`. A click elsewhere that closes the chat keeps its own focus.
- **The live server's headers (BL-212):** every response sends `frame-ancestors 'none'`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `nosniff` and `no-referrer`. `/health` is `{"ok": true}` with no cross-origin header; a dashboard opened from a file finds the server with a `no-cors` request. The launcher asks with `X-Creaite-Vault` whether the server is its own vault's.
- **Milestone strip contrast (BL-213):** the strip's words (months, State and range labels, the details toggle) are `--muted`; `--faint` is for marks only. Menu section headings too.
- **Field names (BL-214):** each Files filter has a name (Search file names, File type, From, To); a Settings `row()` ties its label (`aria-labelledby`) and hint (`aria-describedby`) to its field, or names each of several fields "Label: example". Placeholders are examples, never the only name. **BL-279 (v1.8.0):** `sw()` sets no `aria-label`, so a Settings switch is named by its row's label and described by its hint, never by its settings key.
- **The message and Undo (BL-215):** 10 seconds with Undo, 5 without; held while the pointer is on it or focus is in it (the Undo line pauses), the full time again on leaving; Esc hides it (on it: at once; elsewhere: when nothing else is open to close). ⌘Z (Ctrl+Z) in Work, outside text fields, undoes the last change, as in Review; "Nothing to undo" when there is none.

## Decisions needed (BL-210, sprint-decisions-needed)

What waits on a named person, how long, and what slips. One list and one row, in the page's marked block `/* ===== BL-210 (sprint-decisions-needed) ===== */`, drawn in three places; no new HTML sink and no new wrapper (the rows go in through `putHtml` and the existing project-page and Prep blocks). Internal; reads only; nothing is written to the tracker.
- **The list (`dnList(filter)`):** pending decisions with a decision maker (`by`) and a needed-by date (`due`), `dnOk`. Order (`dnSort`): yours first (`isMe(by)`), then by needed-by date (the latest first, then the soonest), then ID.
- **The data:** `build_dashboard.decision_waits` adds to each pending decision `since` (the earlier of the change log's first insert and the date in its source, the meeting or message that raised it; empty when neither is known) and `slips` (the IDs it blocks, from `links`). Derived each build, never stored.
- **The row (`dnHtml(d, {n, inProject, extra})`):** the decision (opens it); "Yours" (a pill) and "asked of you", or "from <decision maker>"; the project by name (left out on its own page); "waiting N days" from `since` ("raised today" for 0; nothing when unknown); "needed by DD-MM-YYYY, in N days" or, late, "N days late" in red. **What slips** (`dnSlips`): "If it stays open:" the open items it blocks (ID, text, due date) and, after them, the milestone each belongs to, and any open milestone it blocks itself; closed, cancelled, made, withdrawn and done items are left out. With no link recorded the line is not shown, never guessed. Buttons: **Decide** (yours; `recordFromBoard`, the peek with Made staged, closing the Prep first) or **Chase** (others'), and **Open**. With `n` it is a Today line (`tl`), otherwise a `.dnrow` div. Dates never wrap (`.dt`).
- **Chase (`dnChase`):** `openDraftEmail(decision maker, mode, id)`, BL-224's builder: mode `chase` once the decision is late, `update` before; the new `only` argument starts only that decision ticked (still subject to the outside-address rule, so for an address outside your own domains an internal decision starts unticked), the person's other items offered unticked; the mode buttons keep it. A decision maker not on the People page gets a message instead. Nothing is sent and nothing is recorded unless **I sent it** is pressed, as before. With nothing ticked a chase's subject is the plain "Reminder" (no "0 overdue items").
- **Today:** `#dnSec`, a `.tsec` ahead of Needs attention, hidden when the list is empty; the count reads "3 decisions · 1 yours · 1 late"; five show, then **+n more** (`capped`, key `dn`). Needs attention keeps its one-line count of all pending decisions (including undated ones, which the band leaves out).
- **Project page:** the existing Decisions needed card (`#decCard`) draws the project's rows first (`dnBlock`, without the project), then the pending decisions that have no decision maker or no date in the old one-line form, then To revisit.
- **Prep:** in a steering meeting (`isSteering`: "steer" in the title) the brief's Decisions needed section draws the rows (in the list's order, the invite's reason under a row it ranks), then the rest in BL-113's lines; any other meeting is unchanged.
- **Not built:** the pattern notice ("Add as a portfolio risk" when one cause sits behind several slipped milestones) waits for labels (BL-125 stage C).
## Sprint ask-flow (BL-262, BL-257, BL-258, BL-259, BL-261; sprint-ask-flow, v1.7.0)

- **Reopening never redraws (BL-262):** `openChat` never clears `#chLog`; an empty log is filled from the history in one step (`loadChat`) or greeted at once (`greet`, never typed out). The page asks for numbered events with the `X-Creaite-Seq: 1` header (each event then carries `seq`, 1 for the first; other readers get them unnumbered, as before). `SHOWN` is the run on screen and the last event drawn. A reattach (`attachRun` → `attachDraw`) to that run adds nothing and follows from `SHOWN.seq` (`/api/ask/attach {run, after}`); to another run it draws the chat's history at once, including the run's part as far as `/api/chat`'s `seq[run]` says the history reaches, and follows from there. A history without `seq` (an older server) leaves the run out and follows it from the start. A finished run is never replayed: its remaining events (a card, a confirmation) are drawn with no working line (`askStream(…, ended)`). Only new text streams in, with BL-136's fade.
- **Queued messages (BL-257):** Enter during a run (`RUN` set) sends `/api/ask/queue {op:"add", message, images}`; the server keeps one queue with its chat in `system/staging/ask/queue/queue.json` (`{chat, items:[{id, text, images, at}], held}`, at most 20). `#chQueue`, under the log (never inside it), lists each with a **Queued** tag, **Edit** (removes it and puts its text in the box) and **Remove**; the head says when it runs, or that it waits for an answer, with **Run now** (`op:"resume"`) whenever nothing is running. When a run ends the server starts the next item itself (`queue_after`), in the queue's chat with its latest session, before the run is marked done, and names it in the done event (`next`); the page follows it (`attachRun`) with Alan's message added to the log on screen. A run that ends with a question (its last words end with "?"), a confirmation, a card (BL-141's or BL-258's) or an error holds the queue (`held`); his own next run, an answered card or **Run now** lets it go on. `/api/ask/stop` clears it and returns `cleared`; the log says "Stop cleared the queue: n queued messages were removed". A queued message's images are kept with it (and not cleaned up). If the run has just ended as Enter is pressed (`idle`), the message is sent as usual once the page has let go.
- **A tracker change on one card (BL-258):** the run writes cx-edit's spec as `system/staging/drafts/<name>.json` and ends with `CHANGE: <file> | <title>` (stripped from the text). The server snapshots the file (a `.json` directly in drafts, 1 MB at most; else an `editcard` event with `refused`), deletes the draft, runs `propose.py preview` on the snapshot (from `system/guard/`, which only it writes) and emits `editcard {card, title, lines, problems, valid, clash, expires}`. `.edcard` shows the title, a line saying nothing is written yet, each preview line (`ID · Field: old → new` as the field, the old value struck through (`.eddel`, `--bad` on `--bad-soft`) and the new one marked (`.edadd`, `--ok` on `--ok-soft`), each 4.5:1 in every theme), any problems and any clash (a pending, unapplied proposal for the same item). Buttons: **Reject** and **Approve**; **Reject** only when it has problems; **Reject** and **Stage for review** when it clashes, or after Approve answers `busy` (another job holds the lock) or `clash`. `/api/edit/approve` stages the snapshot (`source_run: "ask creaite"`) and runs `tracker.py apply <that proposal>` only; a failed apply removes the proposal (nothing written, nothing left staged) and says why; held items (changed meanwhile) wait in Review. The result names the IDs written, with **Undo** (`/api/undo {apply_id}`), and the dashboard data is reloaded. `/api/edit/reject` stages nothing; `/api/edit/stage` stages it undecided for Review. Used once, 30 minutes, a later card replaces one still waiting, `/api/edit/pending` brings it back after a reload. Recodes and settings never go on it. BL-141's card is unchanged and separate.
- **Quips (BL-259):** 150 in `QUIPS`. `quipNow` deals from a shuffled deck (`QDECK`; never the same twice running across decks) and keeps each for a random 5 to 10 s (`QNOW.until`); `working()` shows the current one, so a new working line keeps a quip until its time is up; the page looks twice a second and changes the text only when it is due. Italic, `aria-hidden`, "Working" for screen readers, as before.
- **Coloured dots (BL-261):** `.mdots i` take `--mdot-1` (`--accent`), `--mdot-2` and `--mdot-3` (`color-mix` of the accent with 14% and 28% of `--surface`), set once on `:root`; each is 3:1 or more on the panel in all nine themes, light and dark (every accent is 5.5:1 or more). The animation and reduced motion are unchanged.

## Sprint ui-sweep (BL-216, BL-217, BL-273, BL-274, BL-270; sprint-ui-sweep, v1.7.0)

Measured against `dev/archive/sprints/ui-sweep-audit.md`; marked blocks `===== BL-2xx (sprint-ui-sweep) =====`, CSS at the end of the stylesheet. Tests: `dev/tests/test_ui_sweep.py` (with `ui_probe.js`, the audit's measuring helpers) and `dev/tests/test_cmd_enter.py`.
- **Targets (BL-216, WCAG 2.5.8):** every pointer target is 24 by 24 or more, or its 24-pixel circle touches no other target. A small drawn control keeps its look: padding grows the hit area and an equal negative margin gives the space back, so nothing moves; a Settings switch answers 24 high through a transparent `::before`. A tick box in Review, Intake or Activity keeps its 13 or 16 pixels and answers a click anywhere in the 24 by 24 square round it, inside its cell (`TICK24`, one capture-phase click handler that clicks the box). Kept under 24 with an equivalent: the column resize handles and ask creaite's grip (arrow keys), the flow chart's bars (the card's table).
- **Reflow (BL-217, WCAG 1.4.10):** at 320, 640 and 900 px nothing scrolls the page or the view sideways; data tables scroll inside their frame. Grids use `min(…,100%)`; Work's tabs wrap under 640; Review's rows stack under 640 (`tr` a grid: the tick, the item, then the buttons on their own line); under 480 the rail is icons only (48 px, `--rail-w`, names kept as titles), Today's attention buttons go under their title, the "more columns" cue is its arrow alone, Settings rows stack.
- **Forced colours (BL-217, WCAG 1.4.11, 2.4.7):** one `@media (forced-colors: active)` block: buttons, chips, segmented switches and badges get a `ButtonText` edge; `[aria-selected]`, `[aria-pressed]`, `.fsel.on`, `.fbtn.on` and the Files tree's row show in `Highlight`/`HighlightText`; unselected tab underlines are `Canvas`; the rail's current page keeps its bar; health sparks, dots and badges keep their colours (`forced-color-adjust:none`); ⌘K's field and rows show focus in `Highlight`. `prefers-contrast: more` makes `--faint` `--muted` and darkens `--line`.
- **The wordmark (BL-273):** `.rail .logo` holds an inline SVG (`svg.wm`, `role="img"`, `aria-label="creaite"`, view box `7 25 306 60`): the word's outlined path once in `<defs>` (`#wmD`), used once as the word (`.wmw`, fill `--ink`), and the bar (`.wmb`, fill `--saffron`). BL-308 removed the dashed draft copy (`.wmd`) and with it BL-291's `prefers-contrast: more` rule. `--saffron` is `#F2B544` on dark rails and `#C7860E` on light ones (every light rail is white, where #F2B544 is 1.8:1), 3:1 or more in every palette. Width `min(62px, rail − 10px)`. Retro Arcade and Thermo Scan hide the SVG and show `.lt`, the text, with their own rules. High contrast draws the word and the bar in `CanvasText`. The source is `dev/reference/logo/creaite-wordmark.svg`.
- **⌘↵ in every editor (BL-274):** one capture-phase handler runs the first registered editor whose `when(target)` holds (`onCmdEnter(name, when, save)`, list `CMDENTER`): `peek` (`pkSaveAndClose`), `panel` (`saveAndClose`, BL-78), `milestone` (`mvSaveAndClose`), `rebaseline` (`mvRebaseline`) and `decide` (`mvAdjust`). With a picker open (a combo, `#mvCal`, `#mvPop`, a calendar) the key is left to the picker and the save follows. `mvSaveAndClose`: a new milestone goes through `mvAddCommit` (its checks; a failure stays open); a pending state or forecast change through its confirm (a missing reason focuses that field); a typed field is blurred, which commits it; a client switch still waiting for wording focuses `#mvCw`; then it closes. Save buttons carry `data-key` ⌘↵.
- **Today's header (BL-270):** `#dayClose` is a round 32 by 32 `.dcbtn` with the `door` icon at the band's bottom right (16 px from the edge under 640, where the band keeps 58 px below the meetings). `dcText()` gives its name and tooltip in three states (last closed, the nudge after `day.close_from`, closed); `.nudge` shows `.dcdot`, `.closed` takes the accent. It opens `openClose`. "Calendar as read on …" is `#calFoot`, a right-aligned faint footnote at the end of `#v-today` (the date in `--warn`), set by `calFoot(cal)` from `renderDay`, shown only when the calendar shown is from an earlier day.

## ask creaite's changes through the dashboard's own actions (BL-314 routing; v191-ask-routing, v1.9.1)
- **Guard decision (Alan, directly, 14-10-2026):** a change asked in ask creaite runs through the dashboard's own action (same route, card, Undo). The model gains no command; BL-141's guard and BL-267's record gate are unchanged.
- **EDIT lines:** for fields of existing actions, RAID items and decisions, the run ends its reply with one `EDIT: <item ID> | <field> | <new value>` line per field (at most 10; stripped from the text by `strip_confirm`). Fields: actions description, owner, priority, due date, status, latest update; RAID description, owner, response, due date, impact, likelihood, status, type; decisions decision, made by, date made, status, needed by, reasoning (named by the dashboard key, the tracker column, the label or a plain word: "done" is Closed, "deadline" is the due date).
- **Server (`build_field_card`, in `_end_answered`, guarded runs only):** reads each item (`tracker.py read`), resolves the field and value (dates DD-MM-YYYY or YYYY-MM-DD and real, choices in their own spelling, an owner against the people register), applies the dashboard's rules (a closed status fills `closed`, reopening clears it; a made decision can't go back to Pending, Made fills today's date, a made decision keeps its date; a title can't be emptied), runs `propose.py preview` on the result from `system/guard/` and emits `fieldcard {card, title, valid, lines, problems, notes, items:[{id, k, patch, label}]}`; `items` is empty unless valid. A value the item already has is a note; only notes gives a "Nothing to change" line and no card. Nothing is staged or written, and there is no new route.
- **Page (`fieldFlow` in ask.js):** `.edcard[data-fcard]` with the preview's lines (`edLine`), problems and notes; **Cancel** and **Go ahead** (only Cancel when it isn't valid). Go ahead calls `itemsMove` (work.js; the Work bulk bar's save, `batchMove`, generalised to a patch per item): `ownerFinal` per owner, `prepMove` (the sidebar's checks: `norm`, `validate`), one `save()` → one proposal through `/api/fs` (`source_run: "dashboard"`), applied through `/api/apply` with auto-apply, one Undo (`data-edundo` with `D.latest_apply`). Items left out are named.
- The CHANGE card (BL-258) is unchanged and stays for what this doesn't cover. A field card isn't kept over a reload (nothing is written without it; ask again).

## One design scale (BL-325; v1.9.5)

Alan's three choices (14-10-2026): one heading pattern in sentence case; primary actions keep their treatment; snapshots re-recorded in one pass with a before-and-after view. Note: `dev/V195-DESIGN-SCALE-NOTE.md`.
- **The scale** is one marked block near the top of `style.css` (`===== BL-325 scale`), shared by all nine palettes, and copied into the two guides by `dev/tools/build_guides.py`. Type `--fs-2xs` 10, `--fs-xs` 12, `--fs-sm` 13, `--fs-md` 14, `--fs-lg` 16, `--fs-xl` 18, `--fs-2xl` 22 and `--fs-display` 34 (Today's day title and Help's hero). Radius `--r-sm` 4, `--r-md` 8, `--r-lg` 12, `--r-xl` 16, `--r-pill`; circles stay `50%`. Shadow `--shadow-sm` (hints, tooltips), `--shadow-md` (menus, popovers, calendars), `--shadow-lg` (a dragged ghost) and `--shadow-side` (the side panels); a theme's `--shadow-card` and `--shadow-pop` stay. Named z-index layers, lowest first: `--z-under` 0 to `--z-local` 5 inside a component, then `--z-menu` 30, `--z-panel` 40, `--z-panel-over` 41, `--z-chat` 45, `--z-badge` 46, `--z-stage` 47, `--z-pop` 60, `--z-pop-over` 65, `--z-drag` 80, `--z-viewer` 90, `--z-tip` 200, `--z-top` 210, `--z-toast` 220 (v1.9.5 check 19: a toast is over everything, menus included; inside a modal it sits in the dialog, BL-316). Spacing stays in px on a 4px base: 2px steps to 16, 4px steps to 32, 8px steps above; 1px nudges and em, ch, % or calc() spacing are the listed exceptions.
- **Controls:** `--ctl-h` 32, `--ctl-r` (`--r-md`; Brightwater sets `--r-sm` for all its controls), `--ctl-fs` (`--fs-sm`) for `.btn`, `.ib` (every icon button is 32 square; `.ib.sm` keeps only its 16px icon), `.dbtn` and every page field and select (padding 0 10px); `.mini` is the compact button at `--ctl-h-sm` 26 with the same radius; Files' centred search is the one large variant (`--ctl-h-lg` 40, `--ctl-r-lg` 10, beside the 40px ask creaite button); `.linkbtn` is 13px in the text face wherever it sits; `.tag` is 12px in the text face. `h1` is 22px on every page (34px on Today and Help's hero); `h2` is 16px/22px in every page, Help keeps its reading scale (22px headings on 16px text) on the same tokens.
- **Section headings:** Today's labels (`h2.th`) are the shared h2, sentence case, in the theme's heading weight (BL-79's bold 13px capitals are gone). Intake's three sections are plain h2s with no icon, named **Calendar import**, **Upload files** and **Quick capture** everywhere (Help, the rules, Settings, the skills, the code's comments).
- **Primary actions** keep their treatment (Today's and Intake's filled button, Review's outlined Approve, Work's tinted "+"); only size and radius are shared.
- **Touch targets:** checkboxes are drawn 16px inside a 24px target (a transparent 4px border; the system's own checkbox under forced colours); the Settings and milestone switches are 40 by 24; the column-resize handle is 24 wide; `.linkbtn` and People's sort buttons answer 24 high.
- **Colours:** the literals that equalled a token are tokens (`--brand-ink` for Brightwater's Charcoal on Signal Green and Linear's white on indigo, `--brand-mark` for Retro Arcade's yellow rail icon, `--btn-ink` and `--accent-ink` where those matched); `--day-ink`, `--day-muted` and `--day-rule`, equal to `--ink`, `--muted` and `--line` in every palette and mode, are aliases set once on `:root`.
- **Projects:** the next milestone's relative date in brackets is lower case ("Saturday 7th November (in 24 days)").
- **Offline page:** creaite's colours (light and dark, accent #5FC4D6 in dark), its fonts and the type and radius scale.
## The one editor (BL-301 A; v20-one-editor, v2.0.0)

- **One editor everywhere text is edited in place:** `src/core/editor.js` `creaiteEditor(el, {features, markdown | html | text, multiline, imageSrc, editorProps, extensions, prop})`, on Tiptap 3 (ProseMirror) vendored and pinned in `system/templates/vendor/tiptap/` (built from `dev/tiptap/` by `dev/tools/tiptap.py`; bundled into the page as `"tiptap"`). ProseMirror mounts on the element given, so its id, classes, `data-…` and `aria-…` stay; each change fires `input` on it as a contenteditable did, so each place's own handlers, save path and validation are unchanged. Tiptap's stylesheet isn't injected; the essentials are in `style.css`.
- **Markdown (`"current"`: Current state's two panes):** loaded by Tiptap's Markdown parser, which keeps each text's written form, and saved by creaite's exact writer (`markdownOf`): what was read is written back byte for byte, an untouched pane is given back as it came, and what is new is written in creaite's style (`_italic_`, `**bold**`, `- `, 2-space nesting). The line ending and blank lines around the text are kept around it (`envelope`). HTML comments (the pane marker, the template's guidance) are kept by a hidden node. BL-207's sentence marks are a ProseMirror mark (`ClaimMark`, `span.claim[data-c]`), never written; BL-63's pictures are the image node drawn as `figure.cimg` with an editable caption (its alt text) and ×. The panes' editors are kept across re-renders (`shared.CURED.ed1`, `ed2`; `curDrop()` ends editing), so Undo history survives. Toolbar: Tiptap commands (`RTE_RUN`); paste keeps only Current state's formats (`transformPastedHTML` through `htmlToMd`/`mdHtml`).
- **Plain text (`"plain"`: the peek's title, latest update, response or reasoning, options, revisit and client messaging; Prep's notes, questions and the popup's shaped notes):** paragraphs only, one a line, no Markdown or marks, so what is stored stays plain text as every other screen and report shows it. `multiline: false` for a one-line field. `prop: "textContent"` (the peek) or `"value"` (Prep, through the `TextEditor` block) makes that property of the element read and set the field's text, so the code that reads it is unchanged.
- **The gate (Alan, 13-10-2026):** every Current state round-trips byte for byte before any editor uses the one editor: `dev/tests/test_one_editor_gate.py` runs `dev/tools/tiptap_roundtrip.py` (groups current, fixtures, hard) on the vendored module and every sample and fixture pane through `creaiteEditor`. A Tiptap upgrade re-runs it.
- **Not switched, on purpose for now (option (a), pending Alan's decision):** the Draft email preview (`#dePrev`, drafts.js) stays a plain `contenteditable`. It is styled HTML for Outlook (inline styles, a table of actions per owner), not Markdown, and Copy email puts that HTML on the clipboard; Tiptap's schema keeps neither, so switching it would change what is copied. Option (b), a Tiptap "email" feature set that keeps `style` and a table node, waits for Alan (`dev/V20-ONE-EDITOR-NOTE.md`).
- **The building block:** `src/components/TextEditor.jsx` (`TextEditor`, COMPONENTS.md § 3), tested on its own by `dev/tests/test_block_texteditor.py` with `creaiteEditor` itself.

## The capture drawer (BL-301 C; v20-capture, v2.0.0)
- **Where:** `src/components/notes/CaptureDrawer.jsx` (the drawer, `openCapture`, `closeCapture`, `captureDone`, `openNoteFull`), `src/stores/capture.js` (`capOpen`), the server's `capture.py` (a library, no command line) and `/api/notes/capture/*` in `live_server.py` (dispatched with `DRAFT_ROUTES`). Replaces FR-71's Quick capture form on Intake.
- **Opening:** ⌘⇧N (Ctrl+Shift+N) on any page, before the typing check, so it works in a text field too; **N** on its own (Alan, 14-10-2026), after the typing check and not while a dialog is open, because ⌘⇧N is Chrome's "new incognito window" in an ordinary tab and may never reach the page (N is used nowhere else: not by Chrome, macOS or creaite's keys; it is in Help's keyboard table and on no screen); ⌘K "New note" (matches "note", "new note", "quick capture"); "Note for this meeting" (`.mnote` under every non-private meeting on Today's band, any day, through `DAYACTS.note`). The page doesn't move. A non-modal `aside#capDrawer` (`role="dialog"`, named by its heading "New note" or "Note for this meeting"), fixed at the right, `min(440px,100vw)` wide, above ask creaite (z-index 47). Focus goes to the note; Esc or × closes it, keeps the draft and puts focus back where it was. It is listed in `OPENERS`, so working in it never closes the peek.
- **Fields:** Project (a native `select`: Unfiled, then the projects by name) with the reason beside it (`#capWhy`); for a meeting, one line "Working session · DD-MM-YYYY · with …"; Title (optional: else the body's first eight words); **Confidential** (`#capConf`, a `.switch` checkbox with `role="switch"`, 24 px high; off for every new note, Alan 14-10-2026), with "Kept out of prep pages and client reports; Claude reads it only if you say yes" beside it when on; the note (`#capBody`, the one editor with features `notes`). The foot: the status (`role="status"`: "Saved as you type", "Draft saved HH:MM", or why not), **Done and find updates**, **Done** (⌘↵; ⌘⇧↵ is Done and find updates), then **Recent notes** (drafts not saved yet, then the five newest notes across the active projects and Unfiled).
- **The guess** (`capture.guess`, `/api/notes/capture/guess`, 600 ms after the last key, until Alan picks a project): cx-minutes' matching rules over the register. Strong: the code as a whole token (longer codes first) or a Teams channel. Medium: the project's name, an alias, a stakeholder listed under that project only (a first name counts when only that project's stakeholders have it), a sender, a client domain, two or more keywords. One keyword is weak and assigns nothing; Shared names (and the words inside them) are never a signal; an Exclude word rules the project out; signals for two projects are a conflict. Only active projects. The reason: "guessed from “Omar Ali” and “data access”", "could be A or B: choose the project", "only “MVP” points to a project: not enough to guess", or "nothing in the note names a project yet". Picking a project shows "chosen by you" and stops the guess; a meeting's project shows "from the meeting's title".
- **Saved as you type:** 600 ms after a pause, `/api/notes/capture/draft` keeps `{id, code, title, body, template, date, with, confidential}` in `system/staging/drafts/notes/capture-<id>.json` (written whole; outside the change stream's stamps, so typing never reloads the page). A drawer opened from a template and left untouched keeps no draft. **Discard** removes it (`/discard`).
- **Done** (`/api/notes/capture/done`): the note is the template's front matter (`notes.template`: title, created, updated, tags, pinned, confidential; a Working session's `date` and `with`) over the typed body, with `confidential: true` when the switch is on (only a JSON `true` sets it: the way notes in `08 Notes` record it, so `notes.is_confidential`, search's `file_passages`, Prep and client output treat it as confidential material), created through `filechange.perform` (`via: "dashboard"`: backed up, logged, undoable) as `<notes folder>/<title>.md` in the project (Unfiled: `_portfolio`), a taken name getting " (2)". The draft is removed once the file is written. When the change has to wait (an ask creaite run has read outside content, so `reach` marks it sensitive) nothing is written, the draft stays, and the drawer says to press Done again later.
- **Done and find updates** saves, then `openNoteFull({code, path, find: true})`, which opens the note through B's own way to a note (`openNoteAt` in `notes.js`, with the project and `find`): the project's Notes tab (Unfiled: the portfolio's notes) with that note open and Find updates (part E) beside it; the address is `#notes=ABC&n=<file name>&find=1` (Unfiled `#notes=_portfolio&n=…`), so a reload keeps it. A recent note in the drawer opens the same way with `find: false` (the note open, no Find updates).
- **Note for this meeting** (`/api/notes/capture/start {meeting: {day, start, title}}`): the Working session template, the meeting's title and day, its attendees from the saved calendar without Alan (none for a confidential meeting), and the project `prep_match` finds (remembered series first). A private meeting has no Note.
- **"note: …" in ask creaite** is handled by the page, never the model (BL-314's routing and the guard are untouched): `/done` with `code: "auto"` (the server guesses; no guess is Unfiled), then one line in the chat naming the note, its project and the reason.
- **Without the live server:** the drawer opens and keeps the text in the page; Done says it needs the live server.
- **Tests:** `dev/tests/test_capture_drawer.py` (the guess, the routes, the drawer in Chrome: ⌘⇧N from Work, the guess and its reason, the choice that sticks, Done through filechange.py, Esc keeps the draft and focus returns, ⌘⇧N carries on, Unfiled, recent notes, Done and find updates and a recent note opening the note in the Notes tab (with Find updates for the first), Note for this meeting, "note: …", ⌘K "New note", N and not while typing, the Confidential switch (off, saved, the route's strict `true`), 320 px and dark).

## Notes on the project page (BL-301 B, v20-notes-tab, 14-10-2026)

- **Pages and addresses:** the project page has tabs (the Tabs block, `components/project/ProjectTabs.jsx`): **Overview** (the cockpit, `#cockpit=ABC`) and **Notes** (`#notes=ABC&n=<file>`), each its own view (`#v-cockpit`, `#v-notes`), so the cockpit's re-renders never touch an open note. There was no Meetings tab to follow, so Notes comes after Overview. Portfolio notes: `#notes=_portfolio`, from **Portfolio notes** on the Projects page. The tab title is "<Project> notes".
- **Header (#115):** the title row is one line (title, Saved, Find updates, ⋯); under it one property line (Created, `NoteDate`: a typed-or-picked date read by `core/page.js` `readDate`, with the pop-up's "Reads as …" line and a calendar; With) and the tags line (`NoteTags`, `components/notes/NoteHeader.jsx`: chips added, renamed and removed by position in the note as it is now; the same `tags` field as before). + New note is Work's `NewTile` (`.newb`); the list's collapse control is a quiet double chevron at the right of NOTES. Focus mode is paused (branch `notes-115-focus-paused`).
- **Screen:** `components/notes/NotesPage.jsx` (list, note, right column, New note dialog on the Dialog block, ⋯ on the Menu block), `components/notes/NoteEditor.jsx` (the one editor, `features: "notes"`), state in `stores/notes.js`, glue in `notes.js`. Grid 260 / 1fr / 240 px; « collapses the list to 40 px (`creaite-notes-list-open` in localStorage; ⌘\); under 1100 px the right column goes below, under 700 px one column.
- **Saving:** a draft (`/api/notes/draft`) one second after the last key, in `system/staging/drafts/notes/<16 hex>.json`; the file (`/api/notes/save`, `filechange.perform` edit, via `dashboard`) on leaving the note or the page (`noteFlush`, `pagehide` with `keepalive`), on hiding the window, or after 30 s idle. A save sends the text's fingerprint (`base`); a file changed since is never overwritten (conflict; the draft is kept). Unchanged: nothing written. A run of ask creaite that has read outside content makes the save wait (kept as a draft, tried again).
- **Pickers:** `[[` notes (this project's first), `@` the people register (never labelled VIP), `#` the project's items (open first), `/` the insert menu (step B: the shared `components/SlashMenu.jsx`, see below). A listbox driven from the editor (`aria-activedescendant` on the text box; ↑ ↓ Enter Tab Esc). Written forms: `[[Title]]`, `@[Name](creaite:person/<name>)`, `[ID](creaite:item/ID)`.
- **Drawn, never written:** ProseMirror decorations (`Decoration`, `DecorationSet`, now exported by the vendored Tiptap module) for note links (`.wl`, `.wl.missing`), item chips (`.nchip` ok/warn/bad from the tracker) and people (`.nper`). Click opens; ⌥-click edits.
- **Data:** `D.notes` (`build_dashboard.notes_data`: each note's front matter and references, never text) and `D.note_templates`. The peek's `#pkNotes` ("n notes mention this") reads `D.notes`. Files lists `notes` as its own folder (the portfolio's too). Search's Notes group opens the note in its tab.
- **Gaps closed (v20-notes-gaps):** **Image** on the toolbar (`data-nt=image`, a hidden file input) sends each PNG or JPEG (2 MB at most) to `/api/notes/image`, which creates it in `<notes folder>/_images/YYYY-MM-DD-<words>.<ext>` through `filechange.perform` (via `dashboard`; a taken name gets `-2`); the figure is BL-63's (`figureView`), written `![caption](_images/<name>)`. `/api/notes/get` returns `images` ({ref: data URL}) for the editor. A save that changed the title is followed by `/api/notes/rename {path, base, old}`: the file is moved to the title's name (`paths.free_path`) and `[[old]]` links in every other note are pointed at the new title, each through `filechange.perform`; a note with a draft is listed, not changed (`notesMsg`). `noteRenamed` makes the page rewrite its address. A Working session's date (DD-MM-YYYY, `#noteWsDate`, the page's date-field settling applies) and with (`#noteWsWith`) are fields in the header, saved with the note (`noteMeta`).
- **Find updates (part E, wired on v20-base):** **✦ Find updates** (`#noteFind`, in the header's slot `#noteFindSlot` beside Saved; disabled without the live server, "Find updates needs the live server") flushes the note, then mounts E's panel in the right-hand column: the grid's third column widens to 360 px (`.nsplit.finding`) and the panel takes the place of Mentioned in, Linked work and People until it is closed; Close and Esc return focus to the button. The note's lines (`noteLines`, the page's port of `notes.paragraphs`) are drawn on the editor's blocks as node decorations (`data-p`, the panel's `data-fu-mk`, the hovered `data-fu-hl`), matched to their line by text and in order; never written, never an edit. `#notes=ABC&n=<file>&find=1` opens the panel at once and the address keeps `&find=1` while it is open. A source link `…/08 Notes/<title>.md#p4` opens the note (`NOTE_OPEN.fn` → `openNoteAt`) with that line in view and highlighted (`.nflash`) for 4 seconds.
- **Links and chips:** a click on a note link, an item chip or a person opens it; ⌥-click puts the cursor inside it to edit (Alan, 15-10-2026: kept).
- **Tabs (Alan, 15-10-2026):** no new tabs in v2.0.0: the project page keeps Overview and Notes; Work, Milestones, Meetings and Files stay where they are.
- **Drafts and the backup (Alan, 15-10-2026):** note drafts (`system/staging/drafts/notes/`) go into the daily backup zip and its OneDrive copy with the rest of staging, a confidential note's draft included. Finished notes are project files and stay out of the zip.
## Find updates (BL-301 E; v20-find-updates, v2.0.0)

- **The panel:** `src/components/notes/FindUpdates.jsx` (`FindUpdates`, `mountFindUpdates(path, el, {noteEl, p, onClose, onMarks})`) with its store `src/stores/findUpdates.js` (`fuPath`, `fuState` idle|ask|busy|ready|none|error|done, `fuRows`, `fuMarkers`, `fuHover`, `fuTicked`, `fuOpen`, `fuSaving`, `fuLast`; `fuFind`, `fuConfirm`, `fuTick`, `fuEdit`, `fuApply`, `fuUndo`). The Notes tab (part B) mounts it into the note's right-hand column; it unmounts on Close or Esc. `aside.fu` named by its heading; a polite status line; groups as `section > h4.fu-g + ul.fu-list`; a row is `li.fu-r[data-p]` with a 24 px checkbox (`Include: <type>: <text>`, disabled for a locked row, described by why), the marker (`.fu-mk`, "Line n"), and a button (`.fu-open`, `aria-expanded`) that opens the row's peek (`.fu-peek`: a `dl.fu-fields` of the fields the server lists; text on the one editor, plain; dates DD-MM-YYYY; owners with the people register as a datalist; Esc closes it and returns focus). Footer: **Apply n changes** (`#fuApply`), **Save for later** (`#fuLater`).
- **The note's markers:** with `noteEl`, every element with `data-p="<line>"` gets `data-fu-mk="<n>"` (drawn by CSS `::before`) and `data-fu-hl` when hovered; hovering a `[data-p]` sets `fuHover`. Attributes only, never markup. An editor that draws its own (a ProseMirror decoration) passes `onMarks(markers, hover)` and sets `fuHover` itself. Lines are `notes.paragraphs`: blank-line blocks, each top-level list item its own line.
- **Server:** `/api/notes/find {path, p?, confirm?}` streams like `/api/draft` (`draftAsk` with `url`; Stop through `[data-drstop]`); the run is kind `notes-find`, with **no tools** and every reading tool denied (as BL-286's closed run); a confidential note answers `{confidential: true}` first. The "draft" event's `items` are the rows: `{key, p, fp, kind, group (new|update|milestone|info), type, text, change[{f,label,from,to}], flags[{t,tone,tip}], notes[], fields[{f,label,kind,options,part}], tick, lock (hedged|invalid), invalid, item}`; `quiet` counts lines with nothing in them. `/api/notes/apply {path, rows[{key, fp, item}], mode: apply|later}` rebuilds each row from the note as it is now (a changed line is refused), dry-runs it, and writes one proposal `proposed-…-note.json` (`source_run: "note"`, `note: {path, rows[{n,p,fp}], files[{p,fp}]}`), approved and applied at once by `tracker.py apply <that file>` (one apply; nothing applied → the file is removed), or pending for Review. `/api/notes/undo {path, apply_id}` undoes that apply when it is still the latest and makes its lines unsent again.
- **Margin ticks (BL-301 B):** `/api/notes/get` returns `sent: [{p, ids, text}]` (`notes.sent_ids` on the file's body), and `/api/notes/apply` and `/api/notes/undo` return it again for the note as it is then (the store's `noteSetSent`). The editor's line decorations match each line to a sent one by its text (as `flat` reads it), so a moved line keeps its tick and an edited one loses it, and set `data-sent="<item IDs>"`; CSS draws a ✓ in `--ok` in the note's left margin (`::after`, outside a list's bullet), in every theme. Drawn only; never an edit.
- **Source links:** `srcParse` reads a note's `#p<n>` (`line`, section "line n"); its link carries `data-noteline`, and a click calls `NOTE_OPEN.fn(path, line)` (core/fields.js) when the Notes tab has set it, else opens the file.

## AI on selected text (BL-301 D; v20-ai-select, v2.0.0)

- **Where (#117, v2.1.0):** every `creaiteEditor` given an `ai` option (`{place, project(), confidential?(), key?()}`) whose place the **place table** (`stores/aiEdit.js AI_PLACE_TABLE`, `aiPlaceOn(place)`) switches on. v2.1.0: **`notes`** (the Notes tab's editor: its project, `confidential: () => the open note's front matter`, `key: () => "note:<path>"`; the capture drawer has no AI bar until v2.2.0) and **`current`** (Current state's two panes). **`email`, `plain` and `line`** (the Draft email, the peek's longer fields, its title, Prep's notes and the Prep popup's shaped notes) are built and **OFF**: they show no sparkle, bar or key, and v2.2.0 flips `ai` (`test_ai_117.py` lists every place and asserts on or off). Each row also says `turnInto` (notes only), `formatNotes` (notes and current) and `improveOnly`. A client-safe `client` place (#123) adds one entry to `AI_PLACE_TABLE`, `AI_PLACES`, `AI_GROUPS` and `edit_ai.py` `PLACES`, `FORMATS` and `FORMAT_RULES`.
- **The sparkle, the bar (`AiSpark`, `AiBar`, `components/AiSelect.jsx`):** a non-empty selection shows a **sparkle** in the editor's left margin (a button, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls="aiBarBox"`); a click or Alt+/ opens the **bar** under the selection (Esc, or a click elsewhere, folds it). Both are placed with `core/caretPlace.js placeAtCaret` (the `/` menu's placement: fixed, from `coordsAtPos`, flips above at the foot of the window, clamped) in `#aiLayer` (on the body, or inside an open modal dialog). Two zones: **Turn into** (plain, untinted: Text, Heading 1 to 3, Bulleted list, Numbered list, Checklist, Quote; `menuitemradio`, the current kind ticked; instant, **no AI, no run**, by the editor's own commands `edTurnInto` so Current state's reversible heading mapping would apply, a toast with Undo, ⌘Z; the empty paragraph ⌘A also takes is left alone; Notes only, Current state's storage can't hold every kind) apart from the tinted **AI** zone: **Format notes** when the selection is two or more blocks, then the place's groups from `AI_GROUPS` (**Improve**, **Structure** or **Tone**, then **Make into**, which holds part E's `aiAction` items: still the single "Make this an action, a risk, a decision or an update"), then **Ask…** (a box: 400 characters; Enter or ⌘↵ asks, Esc goes back). The group menus (`Menu` with `hover`) open when a mouse points at them and fold ~220 ms after it leaves (a menu near the foot of the window opens upward); a click, Enter or ↓ opens one for the keyboard, with focus in it; ← → Home End move along the bar. Alt+/ opens the bar with the first AI menu open. The bar marks its editor with `data-aibar` (`spark` or `open`) and never sets `aria-expanded`, `aria-haspopup`, `aria-controls` or `aria-activedescendant` on it (the `/` menu's); an input method that is composing is never read; the bar and the `/` menu can't show together (a caret shows no sparkle, a selection closes the `/` menu).
- **Options (#117):** **Format notes** (`notes`: rough notes sorted under headings with bullets, every point, date, number, name and item ID kept; a note's own template headings kept; refused for fewer than two non-empty lines), Rewrite clearly, Shorter, Longer (adds no facts), Make it executive (BL-106's voice, `report.style_guide()`), **Add headings and bold** (was Format nicely), **Pull out the tasks** (was Turn into a checklist; notes only), Summarise at the top, More formal and Warmer (email). **There is no spelling option:** every option but Ask… ends its instruction with "Also correct any spelling, grammar and punctuation, in British English." and the card says "Spelling and grammar fixed too (British English)." `current` has no checklist (the Word reports can't take one); `plain` and `line` no formatting; `line` (a title) asks for one line, and both `edit_ai.py result` and `edReplace` (a `multiline: false` editor) make it one line, so Accept never puts a line break in.
- **Headings per place (#117):** `edit_ai.py FORMAT_RULES` is per place: a **note** may write `#`, `##`, `###` headings; **Current state** is stored shifted by two (`###` to `#####`: `headShift`, a `## ` line ends the `## Current state` section), the AI is given the stored levels and told `###`, `####`, `#####` and never `#` or `##`, and `edit_ai.py result` (`relevel`) and the page (`core/editor.js headsSafe`, in `edReplace`) both turn a stray `#` or `##` line (outside fenced code) into `###` or `####`, so no `## ` line is ever written into Current state.
- **The run:** `POST /api/edit/ai {option, text, code, place, instruction, run}` (token-checked like every POST): `edit_ai.py context` builds the prompt from the selected text, the option and the project's name, people (owners of its open items) and open item IDs, nothing else; Claude runs through the run engine as the kind `edit-ai` with **no tools at all** (`--tools ""`, `--strict-mcp-config`, an empty allow list, the read, write, web, agent and Microsoft 365 tools denied, no `--add-dir`, not guarded, not chat history); one run at a time, Stop; `edit_ai.py result` parses the reply and runs BL-106's plain-code check. The debug log gets the option, the place and the length only.
- **The suggestion (`AiSuggestion`):** a non-modal dialog named by the option: the text, or with **Show changes** a word-by-word `<del>`/`<ins>` comparison (`aiDiff`), the check's notes (any date, number, person's name or item ID lost or added), and Accept, Discard, Try again. Nothing changes until **Accept**, one transaction (`edReplace`; ⌘Z undoes it), refused if the text changed meanwhile. Esc discards with focus back to the text. A confidential text (`confidential()`) asks once per `key()` before anything is sent.
- **Two executive paths (Alan, 14-10-2026):** Make it executive on a selection is this quick route; on the whole Current state (AI ▾ beside Save) it keeps BL-106's full flow with the facts sheet and evidence.
- **Counts only:** `draft-stats.json` feature `edit` (offered, used, rejected) through `/api/draft/stat`; never text. No Settings switch (Alan, 14-10-2026).
- **The seam for part E:** `aiAction({id, name, line?, icon?, places?, run(range, h, ai)})` adds an item to the bar's menu (before "Or say what you want…"); picking it hands over the selection, with no Claude run here.
- **Make this an action, a risk, a decision or an update (D × E, notes only):** `NotesPage.jsx` registers `aiAction` `fu-line` ("Find updates on this line only", icon check, `places: ["notes"]`). Picking it finds the one line the selection is on (`selectedLine(h, range)`: the `data-p` of the block at its start and at its end must be the same, else 0), flushes the note and opens Find updates in the right-hand column with that `p` (E's `/api/notes/find {path, p}` reads that line only). A selection on no line (a picture, a table) or over more than one says "Select text on one line of the note: Find updates looks at that line only" in the snackbar and runs nothing; it never falls back to the whole note. No Claude run on the bar and no counts for the hand-over. <!-- moving: the one-line rule (`selectedLine`) is on v20-ai-select (99dd40e, 9b4b976), not yet on v20-capture, where `lineAt(h, range.from)` is used and a selection with no line falls back to the whole note. -->
- **Tests:** `dev/tests/test_ai_select.py` (the script, the route's argv, every place), `dev/tests/test_block_ai_select.py` (the blocks on a fixture; its part E test counts the items the page already registers) and `dev/tests/test_ai_select_notes.py` (the real Notes tab: the notes options, a tool-less edit run on the selection only with counts and no text, Make this an action on one line, two lines refused, a confidential note asks first).

## Notes in v2.0.0 (BL-301): the whole, and what is still moving

The sections above describe each part where it was built: the one editor (A), the capture drawer (C), Notes on the project page (B), Find updates (E) and AI on selected text (D). This one ties them together, with Alan's decisions of 15-10-2026 and what is still open (the last two bullets). Alan's decisions of 15-10-2026 are all built and landed on `release-200-rehearsal`.

- **The flow:** a thought goes in through the drawer (⌘⇧N or N, ⌘K "New note", Today's **Note** under a meeting, or "note: …" in ask creaite) or straight into the Notes tab; it is a Markdown file in the project's notes folder (`08 Notes`; `notes/` in the default layout; Unfiled and portfolio notes in the portfolio's), written only through `filechange.perform` (backed up, logged, undoable); search indexes it as kind `note` and ask creaite cites it; **✦ Find updates** (the whole note's new lines) or AI › Make this an action (one line) turns lines into rows that Alan ticks and applies as one apply, with Undo; `notes-sent.json` remembers which lines went, never their text.
- **Passive until Find updates:** the sweep, cx-minutes and anything unattended never read a note for the tracker. Quick capture (FR-71) is retired: the drawer replaced its form; quick notes saved before v2.0.0 stay in `minutes/` and cx-minutes still processes them.
- **Confidential notes:** searchable on the laptop; their text is kept out of ask creaite's gathered context (only a count is given), AI on a selection and Find updates ask once before their text goes to Claude, and Find updates labels their new items Sensitive. Every run creaite starts itself is a **held run** (`held_run()`, hook mode `held`, `CREAITE_RUN_KIND`): the transcript check and load, Current state's draft and rewrite, Search's answer, the Prep page, AI drafts, Shape the meeting and Close the day's sweep. The guard hook refuses a confidential note by path or by any tool, and `search.py` in a held run gives no title, link or match for it; `file_passages` (context) fails closed for every caller. ask creaite (Alan's own chat), confirmed runs and the dashboard's in-process search are unchanged. Pinned in `dev/tests/test_guard_pinned.py` (re-pinned in their own commits, 15-10-2026); tests in `test_prep_guard_confidential.py` and `test_prep_confidential_notes.py`.
- **ask creaite and outside content (Alan, 15-10-2026: notes are exempt):** reading a note, a `.md` directly in a project's or the portfolio's notes folder as `notes.note_of` accepts it, doesn't mark an ask creaite run as having read outside content, so a later ordinary change needs no approval card. A note's pictures (`_images/`), every other file in the engagement folder, transcripts, documents and `inbox/` still mark. The marking is `live_server.py` `OUTSIDE_CONTENT` and the hook in `reach.py`; no other guard change in this release.
- **Margin ticks:** a line already sent shows a ✓ in the note's left margin, and editing the line takes it away.
- **The real-data gate:** no release until every one of Alan's real Current state panes round-trips byte for byte through the one editor. <!-- moving: plan step 6, Alan with Team Cap on laptop 1, read only (`tiptap_roundtrip.py --private --exact`); on 13-10-2026 3 of 4 panes passed, the fourth holding only an HTML comment, which the comment node is to close. -->
- **Still open from A:** the Draft email preview stays a plain `contenteditable` (option (a)). From the integration note: once a note with a mixed list (bullets with one checklist item) is edited, the exact writer saves the checklist item as its own list; markers and sent lines still work, but the file's layout changes on the first edit.

## The shared editor foundation: profiles, toolbar, checklist, headings (#122 step A, 2026-10-16)

- **Profiles** (`core/editorProfiles.js`, `ED_PROFILES`): `notes` (Notes and the capture drawer: H1 to H3, B I S, code, lists, checklist, indent and outdent, quote, link, unlink, clear, `[[` `@` `#`, table, picture, undo, redo), `current` (Current state: the same minus checklist, code, table and `[[` `@` `#`), `plain` (item fields, Prep notes, draft boxes: no toolbar, plain text as before) and `none` (a host that sets its own frame: the report sheets). A tool the host does itself (`host: true`: its pickers, table, file input) shows only if the host supplies it, so the capture drawer shows the shared buttons only. The schema profiles in the vendored Tiptap bundle are unchanged (no bundle rebuild).
- **One bar** (`components/EditorBar.jsx`): Notes (`.nbar`), the capture drawer (`.capbar`) and Current state (`#curBar`, with the legacy `data-rc` names kept) all draw it. `role="toolbar"`, a roving tabindex (one Tab stop), arrow keys, Home, End and wrap, Esc back to the text, Enter or Space runs a button; `aria-pressed` follows the cursor, `aria-disabled` for Unlink away from a link and Indent outside a list; buttons are at least 28 by 26 px and named. A mouse press keeps the cursor in the text. Mounting dispatches nothing, so opening an editor is never an edit.
- **Keys:** ⌘K is the command palette everywhere (Shift or not, outside an editor); inside a rich editor with a bar, ⌘⇧K opens the link form (https, http and mailto only) and ⌘⇧X is strikethrough; both are caught at capture by the bar's module so the palette never sees them. Current state's old ⌘K link key and its wrong tooltip are gone.
- **Checklist:** the 18 px box is centred on the first line of text (`.ProseMirror ul[data-type=taskList]`: the label is one line tall, `1lh`, and centres the 26 px element whose 4 px transparent border is the click target). Every rich host that takes a checklist gets it (Notes today; Current state's checklist is v2.2.0).
- **Headings:** the editor always has H1 to H3. Notes stores `#`, `##`, `###`. Current state stores `###`, `####`, `#####` (`HEAD_SHIFT` 2, `creaiteEditor` option `headShift`; `headsIn` and `headsOut` map the text at load, `value()`, the AI range and replace, and a paste), so a heading can never be a `## ` line that ends the section. The mapping is reversible: any other line that starts with 1 to 6 `#` and a space (a stray `# ` or `## `, `######`) is shown as plain text, with a zero-width space before the `#` on the way in and taken off on the way out, so an untouched line is saved byte for byte; the editor is limited to Heading 1 to 3 here (typed `####` stays text) and a level above 3 is clamped to `#####` on the way out; fenced code and HTML comments are left alone; the real-data gate (`dev/tools/tiptap_roundtrip.py`) runs the same `headsIn` and `headsOut` around the editor (`build_dashboard.py`, `report.py` read to the next `^## `). `docx_tools.md_blocks` and `md_normalise` learn `h2` and `h3`; Word shows the first level in the topic style (as before), the second bold and the third bold italic; `panel.js` `mdBlocks` and `mdHtml` draw `<h3>`, `<h4>`, `<h5>`. Existing `###` text now opens as Heading 1, drawn at the size the sub-heading always had.
- **Not in step A:** the `/` menu and its position fix (step B), item links and `#` text (step C), the selection bar and Turn into (#117), the richer link control (#120), focus mode (#115), plain-profile links and AI (v2.2.0), the report sheets (they set `chrome: none`; `SheetText` is on the v210 branches only).

## The `/` menu at the caret and headings 1 to 3 (#122 step B, 2026-10-16)

- **One menu, from the profile table** (`core/editorProfiles.js`: `ED_SLASH` the rows, `ED_SLASH_ROWS` each profile's rows in order, `slashRows(profile, hostRows, schema, q)` the filter; `components/SlashMenu.jsx` `attachSlash(handle, {profile, host})` draws it). Notes: Text, Heading 1 to 3, Bulleted, Numbered, Checklist, Quote, Code (when the schema has a code block), Table, Image, Work item, Person, Note, and the three templates (a template row only when the host has the template). Current state: Text, Heading 1 to 3, Bulleted, Numbered, Quote, Image (no checklist, code, table, links or templates: its storage can't hold them). Plain and none: no menu. The old "Sub-heading" row (H2) is gone. A row marked `host` is done by the host (its pickers, table, templates, file input) and shows only when the host passes it; the capture drawer attaches the same menu with the `notes` profile and no host rows (see the drawer bullet below).
- **Opens** when a `/` is typed at the start of a line or after a space, in a paragraph (`slashAt`): never inside a word or an address (`and/or`, `https://x/y`, `1/2`), in code or a code block, a link or a heading, nor during an IME composition (`view.composing`, `compositionend` reads the result). It narrows as you type, on a row's id or short alias (`h1` `h2` `h3`, `ul` `bullet`, `ol` `num`, `todo` `check`, `quote` `bq`, `code`, `table`, `img`, also `heading1`, `blockquote`, `checkbox`, `picture`, `photo`, `paragraph`, `plain`, `numbered`, `number`, `bullets`), its name (`heading 2`, `bulleted list`: one space is allowed in the query) or its description, best first (an exact id or alias, then a name or alias starting with it, then a word of the name, then the rest; the menu's order breaks ties; "h" lists the headings first, then every other row with an h in it, as the mockup does; a phrase with a space that names no row closes the menu and stays text), says "No match" when nothing fits, and closes when the caret moves, the text loses focus, Esc is pressed (leaving the typed text; the same `/` does not reopen it until it is deleted) or Enter finds no row. ↑ ↓ move, Enter or Tab insert, a click inserts. Enter removes the "/query" and writes the block (`setHeading`, list toggles, `setParagraph`); a host row first removes the query, then the host acts (`#` types a `#` and the item picker takes over).
- **Headings:** Heading 1 to 3 are the editor's levels; the host's `headShift` writes them (Notes `#` `##` `###`, Current state `###` `####` `#####`). Current state's own document keydown handlers (Esc cancels, Tab indents) stand back while the menu is open (`aria-expanded` on the text).
- **Position (the bug):** the old menu and the `[[ @ #` lists were `position:absolute` inside `.ned` but measured against `.ndoc`, so they dropped by the height of the note header and the formatting bar (about 127 px at 1400) and never flipped. `core/caretPlace.js` `placeAtCaret(view, pos, el)` now places them `position:fixed` from `coordsAtPos` (as `aiPlace` does): 4 px below the caret, above when 360 px do not fit below and there is more room above, the height cut to the room on the roomier side when neither fits, left clamped to 8 px inside the window, re-placed on any scroll (capture) and on resize; `data-side` says which side. `.slashmenu` and `.ntpick` are `z-index:220`, max-height 360 px; the menu is appended to `body` (or the open `dialog`).
- **Rows that match the block you are in (review fixes, editor-122b3):** Bulleted, Numbered (inside a numbered list), Checklist and Quote inside that same kind of block only remove the typed `/query` (a toggle would lift the line out and split the list or quote, and in Current state outdent a nested item); a different list type inside a list converts it. The **Text** row is always a no-op today, because the menu only opens in a paragraph; it stays because the mockup has it. **⌘↵** is held back under an open menu on every host, including Current state (`slashOn` in `projects.js` now covers ⌘↵ as it does Esc and Tab), so the save waits. **Headings inside a quote** in Current state are shifted like any other (`> #` is stored `> ###`, `> > #` as `> > ###`; `headsIn`/`headsOut` keep the quote prefix and a stray `> # x` is protected like a stray `# x`). The menu is re-placed on the next frame and 120 ms after a resize or scroll, so it follows the caret when a host reflows. A polite `role=status` line says "No match" (or the number of options), and on close the editor loses `aria-haspopup` and `aria-activedescendant` and keeps `aria-expanded="false"`.
- **Accessibility:** `role=listbox` (`aria-label` Insert) of `role=group` (the group name) of `role=option` rows (min height 32 px, 24 px icon tile); `aria-activedescendant`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` and `aria-haspopup` on the editor text while open; forced-colours rules for the chosen row; tokens only; no animation.
- **The capture drawer's menu (Alan's decision 5 on #122):** `CaptureDrawer.jsx` attaches the same `attachSlash` with the `notes` profile and no host rows, so it offers what the drawer can do itself: Text, Heading 1 to 3, Bulleted, Numbered, Checklist, Quote and Code (the table and image rows, the Work item, Person and Note rows and the templates need a host that the drawer does not have yet, so they are not offered; the toolbar is the same bar without those buttons). The drawer's text stores `#` `##` `###` like Notes. The menu is a fixed popup in `body` (`z-index:220`, above the drawer's 47) at the caret. Keys: the first Esc closes the menu and leaves the typed text, the second closes the drawer (the drawer's key handler stands back while the text has `aria-expanded`); ⌘↵ is swallowed by the open menu (`SlashMenu` stops it, so neither the editor nor Done acts) and saves once the menu is closed. The `[[` `@` `#` pickers do not exist in the drawer (they live in `NoteEditor.jsx` with the host's own state): step C.
- **Links (#120; rich profiles: Notes, the capture drawer, Current state):** ONE address check, `core/linkUrl.js` (`linkUrl` for an address as stored, `typedLinkUrl` for what a person types, which trims, adds `https://` to a bare domain and `mailto:` to a bare email, and never guesses a scheme; `keepHref` is what a document may keep, which also allows creaite's own `creaite:item/` and `creaite:person/` links). It allows http, https and mailto only and refuses any other scheme in any case mix, a control, space or invisible character anywhere, an entity (`&#106;`, `&colon;`), a backslash, `//host`, a relative address, quotes, angle brackets, a back-tick, a name and password before the host, a mailto list and anything over 2048 characters. `core/editorLink.js` holds the rest: the `linkGuard` plugin (every `creaiteEditor` of a rich profile has it; it strips a link mark whose address `keepHref` refuses, in the same transaction as a paste, a command or a load, and keeps a quiet transaction quiet), `scrubNow` (the document just opened, quietly, so an untouched text is still written back as it came and a hostile address shows as plain text), `pasteLink`, `linkAt`, `applyLink` and `removeLink`. The popover is `components/LinkPopover.jsx` (a labelled dialog, "Link"): the form (Text, Address, Add or Apply, Remove link, Open, Cancel; the cursor starts in Address; Enter applies, Esc cancels and gives the cursor back, Tab stays inside) and the one-line view (address, Open, Edit, Remove; shown by a click on a link or by arriving in one with the arrow keys; Tab from the text reaches it outside a list). A click on a link never navigates; Open is an anchor with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`. Four ways in: the bar's Link button, the `/` menu's Link row (`hook: "link"` in `ED_SLASH`), Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+K (caught by the editor before the palette; Chrome has no shortcut on it), and paste (an address alone becomes a link whose text is the address; over selected words it links them; either way a toast with Undo; anything the check refuses pastes as plain text). A link mark is not inclusive (typing after a link is outside it). Written `[text](address)`, or the bare address when the text is the address. A web link is never written over a work-item or person link. The plain profile (item fields, Prep notes) is #121.
- **Not in step B:** item links and the `#` text (step C), the capture drawer's pickers, table and image (step C), a divider row, keyboard shortcuts for headings beyond the Markdown `#` `##` `###` input rules (already on).
